### **Public-Facing Strings (Not Visible to End Users)**

The term "orchestrator" appears in:
- **SSE events**: No - the `experimental_context` with `actor: "orchestrator"` is internal to the AI SDK, not sent to clients
- **API responses**: No - SSE events use `type: "token"`, `"extraction"`, `"assistant_message"`, `"done"`, `"error"`, `"onboarding_state"`

- **System prompt sent to LLM**: **YES** - "You are the Orchestrator agent..." is sent to the Sonnet model

### **Key Observations**

1. **"Orchestrator" is the architectural role**, not exposed as a public-facing term (except in system prompt to the LLM)
2. **Actor context**: The `actor: "orchestrator"` field in tools distinguishes orchestrator execution from PM agent (`pm_agent`), MCP (`mcp`), and chat agent (`chat_agent`)
3. **Tool registration**: All 14 tools are registered via `createOrchestratorTools()`, though AGENTS.md documentation currently lists 12 (shows-relationship-graph and one other are newer)
4. **Naming consistency**: Uses snake_case in data (`source_agent: "orchestrator"`) and camelCase in types (`actor: "orchestrator"`)
5. **No external visibility**: End users never see the word "orchestrator" in responses, events, or error messages—it's an internal implementation detail

```
     1→import { stepCountIs, streamText, type ModelMessage } from "ai";
     2→import { RecordId, Surreal } from "surrealdb";
     3→import type { ExtractedEntity, ExtractedRelationship, OnboardingState } from "../../shared/contracts";
     4→import { buildChatContext, buildSystemPrompt } from "./context";
     5→import { createOrchestratorTools } from "./tools";
     6→
     7→type ConversationMessage = {
     8→  role: "user" | "assistant";
     9→  text: string;
    10→};
    11→
    12→type CollectedEntity = ExtractedEntity;
    13→type CollectedRelationship = ExtractedRelationship;
    14→
    15→export type OrchestratorResult = {
    16→  text: string;
    17→  collectedEntities: CollectedEntity[];
    18→  collectedRelationships: CollectedRelationship[];
    19→};
    20→
    21→export async function runOrchestrator(input: {
    22→  surreal: Surreal;
    23→  model: any;
    24→  pmModel: any;
    25→  embeddingModel: any;
    26→  embeddingDimension: number;
    27→  extractionModelId: string;
    28→  extractionModel: any;
    29→  extractionStoreThreshold: number;
    30→  conversationRecord: RecordId<"conversation", string>;
    31→  workspaceRecord: RecordId<"workspace", string>;
    32→  currentMessageRecord: RecordId<"message", string>;
    33→  latestUserText: string;
    34→  workspaceOwnerRecord?: RecordId<"person", string>;
    35→  inheritedEntityIds?: RecordId[];
    36→  messages: ConversationMessage[];
    37→  isOnboarding?: boolean;
    38→  onboardingState?: OnboardingState;
    39→  onToken: (token: string) => Promise<void> | void;
    40→}): Promise<OrchestratorResult> {
    41→  const context = await buildChatContext({
    42→    surreal: input.surreal,
    43→    conversationRecord: input.conversationRecord,
    44→    workspaceRecord: input.workspaceRecord,
    45→    ...(input.inheritedEntityIds && input.inheritedEntityIds.length > 0
    46→      ? { inheritedEntityIds: input.inheritedEntityIds }
    47→      : {}),
    48→  });
    49→
    50→  const system = buildSystemPrompt(context, {
    51→    isOnboarding: input.isOnboarding,
    52→    onboardingState: input.onboardingState,
    53→  });
    54→  const modelMessages: ModelMessage[] = input.messages.map((message) => ({
    55→    role: message.role,
    56→    content: message.text,
    57→  }));
    58→
    59→  const result = streamText({
    60→    model: input.model,
    61→    system,
    62→    messages: modelMessages,
    63→    tools: createOrchestratorTools({
    64→      surreal: input.surreal,
    65→      pmModel: input.pmModel,
    66→      embeddingModel: input.embeddingModel,
    67→      embeddingDimension: input.embeddingDimension,
    68→      extractionModelId: input.extractionModelId,
    69→      extractionModel: input.extractionModel,
    70→      extractionStoreThreshold: input.extractionStoreThreshold,
    71→    }),
    72→    experimental_context: {
    73→      actor: "orchestrator",
    74→      workspaceRecord: input.workspaceRecord,
    75→      conversationRecord: input.conversationRecord,
    76→      currentMessageRecord: input.currentMessageRecord,
    77→      latestUserText: input.latestUserText,
    78→      ...(input.workspaceOwnerRecord ? { workspaceOwnerRecord: input.workspaceOwnerRecord } : {}),
    79→    },
    80→    stopWhen: stepCountIs(5),
    81→  });
    82→
    83→  let text = "";
    84→  const collectedEntities: CollectedEntity[] = [];
    85→  const collectedRelationships: CollectedRelationship[] = [];
    86→
    87→  for await (const part of result.fullStream) {
    88→    if (part.type === "text-delta") {
    89→      text = `${text}${part.text}`;
    90→      await input.onToken(part.text);
    91→      continue;
    92→    }
    93→
    94→    if (part.type === "tool-result") {
    95→      const toolResult = part.output as Record<string, unknown> | undefined;
    96→      if (toolResult) {
    97→        if (Array.isArray(toolResult.extracted_entities)) {
    98→          collectedEntities.push(...(toolResult.extracted_entities as CollectedEntity[]));
    99→        }
   100→        if (Array.isArray(toolResult.extracted_relationships)) {
   101→          collectedRelationships.push(...(toolResult.extracted_relationships as CollectedRelationship[]));
   102→        }
   103→      }
   104→    }
   105→  }
   106→
   107→  if (text.trim().length === 0) {
   108→    text = await result.text;
   109→  }
   110→
   111→  return {
   112→    text: text.trim(),
   113→    collectedEntities,
   114→    collectedRelationships,
   115→  };
   116→}
   117→
   118→export const runGraphAwareChat = runOrchestrator;
   119→
```

```
     1→import { tool } from "ai";
     2→import { z } from "zod";
     3→import { confirmDecisionRecord, getDecisionRecordForWorkspace, getWorkspaceOwnerRecord } from "../../graph/queries";
     4→import { requireToolContext, toDecisionRecordId } from "./helpers";
     5→import type { ChatToolDeps } from "./types";
     6→
     7→const CONFIRMABLE_STATUSES = new Set(["provisional", "inferred"]);
     8→
     9→export function createConfirmDecisionTool(deps: ChatToolDeps) {
    10→  return tool({
    11→    description:
    12→      "Confirm a provisional or inferred decision. ONLY use this when the user explicitly approves a decision in conversation. Never call this without clear user authorization.",
    13→    inputSchema: z.object({
    14→      decision_id: z.string().min(1).describe("Decision record ID to confirm"),
    15→      notes: z.string().optional().describe("Additional user context"),
    16→    }),
    17→    execute: async (input, options) => {
    18→      const context = requireToolContext(options);
    19→
    20→      if (context.actor !== "chat_agent" && context.actor !== "orchestrator") {
    21→        throw new Error("confirm_decision is only available for chat_agent or orchestrator context");
    22→      }
    23→
    24→      const decisionRecord = toDecisionRecordId(input.decision_id);
    25→      const decision = await getDecisionRecordForWorkspace({
    26→        surreal: deps.surreal,
    27→        workspaceRecord: context.workspaceRecord,
    28→        decisionInput: `decision:${decisionRecord.id as string}`,
    29→      });
    30→
    31→      if (!CONFIRMABLE_STATUSES.has(decision.status)) {
    32→        throw new Error(`decision is not confirmable from status '${decision.status}'`);
    33→      }
    34→
    35→      const ownerRecord = context.workspaceOwnerRecord
    36→        ? context.workspaceOwnerRecord
    37→        : await getWorkspaceOwnerRecord({
    38→            surreal: deps.surreal,
    39→            workspaceRecord: context.workspaceRecord,
    40→          });
    41→
    42→      const confirmedAt = new Date();
    43→      await confirmDecisionRecord({
    44→        surreal: deps.surreal,
    45→        decisionRecord: decision.id,
    46→        confirmedAt,
    47→        ...(ownerRecord ? { confirmedBy: ownerRecord } : {}),
    48→        ...(input.notes ? { notes: input.notes } : {}),
    49→      });
    50→
    51→      return {
    52→        decision_id: `decision:${decision.id.id as string}`,
    53→        status: "confirmed",
    54→        name: decision.summary,
    55→      };
    56→    },
    57→  });
    58→}
    59→
```

     1→import { type RecordId, type Surreal } from "surrealdb";
     2→import type { ObservationSummary, OnboardingState } from "../../shared/contracts";
     3→import {
     4→  listConversationEntities,
     5→  listWorkspaceOpenQuestions,
     6→  listWorkspaceProjectSummaries,
     7→  listWorkspaceRecentDecisions,
     8→  type ConversationEntity,
     9→  type WorkspaceDecisionSummary,
    10→  type WorkspaceProjectSummary,
    11→  type WorkspaceQuestionSummary,
    12→} from "../graph/queries";
    13→import { listWorkspaceOpenObservations } from "../observation/queries";
    14→import { loadOnboardingSummary } from "../onboarding/onboarding-state";
    15→import { chatComponentSystemPrompt } from "./chat-component-system-prompt";
    16→
    17→export type ChatContext = {
    18→  conversationEntities: ConversationEntity[];
    19→  workspaceSummary: {
    20→    projects: WorkspaceProjectSummary[];
    21→    recentDecisions: WorkspaceDecisionSummary[];
    22→    openQuestions: WorkspaceQuestionSummary[];
    23→    openObservations: ObservationSummary[];
    24→  };
    25→  onboardingSummary?: string;
    26→};
    27→
    28→type ChatContextLoaders = {
    29→  listConversationEntities: typeof listConversationEntities;
    30→  listWorkspaceProjectSummaries: typeof listWorkspaceProjectSummaries;
    31→  listWorkspaceRecentDecisions: typeof listWorkspaceRecentDecisions;
    32→  listWorkspaceOpenQuestions: typeof listWorkspaceOpenQuestions;
    33→  listWorkspaceOpenObservations: typeof listWorkspaceOpenObservations;
    34→  loadOnboardingSummary: typeof loadOnboardingSummary;
    35→};
    36→
    37→export async function buildChatContext(input: {
    38→  surreal: Surreal;
    39→  conversationRecord: RecordId<"conversation", string>;
    40→  workspaceRecord: RecordId<"workspace", string>;
    41→  loaders?: ChatContextLoaders;
    42→  inheritedEntityIds?: RecordId[];
    43→}): Promise<ChatContext> {
    44→  const loaders = input.loaders ?? {
    45→    listConversationEntities,
    46→    listWorkspaceProjectSummaries,
    47→    listWorkspaceRecentDecisions,
    48→    listWorkspaceOpenQuestions,
    49→    listWorkspaceOpenObservations,
    50→    loadOnboardingSummary,
    51→  };
    52→
    53→  const [conversationEntities, projects, recentDecisions, openQuestions, openObservations, onboardingSummary] = await Promise.all([
    54→    loaders.listConversationEntities({
    55→      surreal: input.surreal,
    56→      conversationRecord: input.conversationRecord,
    57→      workspaceRecord: input.workspaceRecord,
    58→      limit: 60,
    59→      ...(input.inheritedEntityIds && input.inheritedEntityIds.length > 0
    60→        ? { inheritedEntityIds: input.inheritedEntityIds }
    61→        : {}),
    62→    }),
    63→    loaders.listWorkspaceProjectSummaries({
    64→      surreal: input.surreal,
    65→      workspaceRecord: input.workspaceRecord,
    66→      limit: 20,
    67→    }),
    68→    loaders.listWorkspaceRecentDecisions({
    69→      surreal: input.surreal,
    70→      workspaceRecord: input.workspaceRecord,
    71→      limit: 12,
    72→    }),
    73→    loaders.listWorkspaceOpenQuestions({
    74→      surreal: input.surreal,
    75→      workspaceRecord: input.workspaceRecord,
    76→      limit: 12,
    77→    }),
    78→    loaders.listWorkspaceOpenObservations({
    79→      surreal: input.surreal,
    80→      workspaceRecord: input.workspaceRecord,
    81→      limit: 10,
    82→    }),
    83→    loaders.loadOnboardingSummary(input.surreal, input.workspaceRecord),
    84→  ]);
    85→
    86→  return {
    87→    conversationEntities,
    88→    workspaceSummary: {
    89→      projects,
    90→      recentDecisions,
    91→      openQuestions,
    92→      openObservations,
    93→    },
    94→    onboardingSummary,
    95→  };
    96→}
    97→
    98→function formatConversationEntities(entities: ConversationEntity[]): string {
    99→  if (entities.length === 0) {
   100→    return "- none";
   101→  }
   102→
   103→  return entities
   104→    .slice(0, 20)
   105→    .map((entity) => `- ${entity.kind}: ${entity.name} (confidence ${entity.confidence.toFixed(2)})`)
   106→    .join("\n");
   107→}
   108→
   109→function formatProjectList(projects: WorkspaceProjectSummary[]): string {
   110→  if (projects.length === 0) {
   111→    return "- none";
   112→  }
   113→
   114→  return projects
   115→    .slice(0, 15)
   116→    .map((project) => `- ${project.name} [id: ${project.id}] active tasks: ${project.activeTaskCount}`)
   117→    .join("\n");
   118→}
   119→
   120→function formatDecisionList(decisions: WorkspaceDecisionSummary[]): string {
   121→  if (decisions.length === 0) {
   122→    return "- none";
   123→  }
   124→
   125→  return decisions
   126→    .slice(0, 15)
   127→    .map((decision) => {
   128→      const project = decision.project ? ` project: ${decision.project}` : "";
   129→      return `- ${decision.name} [id: ${decision.id}] status: ${decision.status}${project}`;
   130→    })
   131→    .join("\n");
   132→}
   133→
   134→function formatQuestionList(questions: WorkspaceQuestionSummary[]): string {
   135→  if (questions.length === 0) {
   136→    return "- none";
   137→  }
   138→
   139→  return questions
   140→    .slice(0, 15)
   141→    .map((question) => {
   142→      const project = question.project ? ` project: ${question.project}` : "";
   143→      return `- ${question.name} [id: ${question.id}]${project}`;
   144→    })
   145→    .join("\n");
   146→}
   147→
   148→function formatObservationList(observations: ObservationSummary[]): string {
   149→  if (observations.length === 0) {
   150→    return "- none";
   151→  }
   152→
   153→  return observations
   154→    .slice(0, 10)
   155→    .map((observation) => {
   156→      const category = observation.category ? `, ${observation.category}` : "";
   157→      return `- [${observation.severity}] ${observation.text} (by ${observation.sourceAgent}, ${observation.status}${category})`;
   158→    })
   159→    .join("\n");
   160→}
   161→
   162→type SystemPromptOptions = {
   163→  isOnboarding?: boolean;
   164→  onboardingState?: OnboardingState;
   165→};
   166→
   167→export function buildSystemPrompt(context: ChatContext, options?: SystemPromptOptions): string {
   168→  const sections: string[] = [
   169→    "You are the Orchestrator agent for a workspace-aware project intelligence system.",
   170→    "You are a **thin orchestrator**: your job is to decide which tools and subagents to invoke, control sequencing, and synthesize user-facing responses.",
   171→    "You have access to a workspace-scoped knowledge graph stored in SurrealDB.",
   172→    "Never assume data from other workspaces.",
   173→    "",
   174→    "## Architecture: Graph as Communication Bus",
   175→    "All agent-to-agent communication happens through the knowledge graph. You do NOT pass data between agents directly.",
   176→    "- Each agent reads the graph for context it needs.",
   177→    "- Each agent writes its outputs (entities, observations, decisions) directly to the graph.",
   178→    "- Subsequent agents read the graph and see prior agents' work.",
   179→    "- You synthesize the user-facing response from graph state and tool results.",
   180→    "",
   181→  ];
   182→
   183→  // Onboarding mode instructions
   184→  if (options?.isOnboarding && options.onboardingState !== "complete") {
   185→    if (options.onboardingState === "summary_pending") {
   186→      sections.push(
   187→        "## Onboarding Mode — Summary",
   188→        "The workspace onboarding is wrapping up. Summarize what has been captured so far and ask the user to confirm or add anything else.",
   189→        "",
   190→        "Current workspace state:",
   191→        context.onboardingSummary ?? "No entities captured yet.",
   192→        "",
   193→      );
   194→    } else {
   195→      sections.push(
   196→        "## Onboarding Mode",
   197→        "You are onboarding a newly created workspace.",
   198→        "Ask one natural question at a time like a smart colleague, never as a form.",
   199→        "Cover these topics over 5-7 turns: business/venture, projects, people, decisions, tools, bottlenecks.",
   200→        "Keep acknowledgment to one sentence max. Ask exactly one concrete follow-up question.",
   201→        "",
   202→        "When the user describes their workspace, create entities directly:",
   203→        "- Projects → dispatch PM agent with plan_work intent",
   204→        "- Decisions → use create_provisional_decision",
   205→        "- Open questions requiring a choice → use create_question (not for informational queries)",
   206→        "- People mentioned → note in your response (person creation is handled separately)",
   207→        "",
   208→        "Current workspace state:",
   209→        context.onboardingSummary ?? "No entities captured yet.",
   210→        "",
   211→      );
   212→    }
   213→  }
   214→
   215→  sections.push(
   216→    "## When to Create Decisions",
   217→    "Commitment/selection language: \"let's go with\", \"we decided\", \"we'll use\", \"going with\", \"settled on\".",
   218→    "Decision vs feature: choice language (X instead of Y) = decision; description language (we need X) = feature.",
   219→    "",
   220→    "## When to Create Questions",
   221→    "Only for open questions that require a choice or pending decision: \"should we use X or Y?\", \"which approach for Z?\".",
   222→    "Do NOT create question entities for informational queries (\"what is blocking X?\", \"how does Y work?\", \"what's the status?\") — answer those directly.",
   223→    "One question per topic; if question includes options (X or Y), create one question entity.",
   224→    "",
   225→    "## When NOT to Create Entities",
   226→    "- Casual conversation, greetings, clarifications, status queries",
   227→    "- Vague references: \"my project\", \"the feature\", \"the thing\"",
   228→    "- User is brainstorming/exploring — wait for convergence",
   229→    "",
   230→  );
   231→
   232→  sections.push(
   233→    "## This Conversation",
   234→    "Entities in this conversation:",
   235→    formatConversationEntities(context.conversationEntities),
   236→    "",
   237→    "## Workspace Overview",
   238→    "Projects:",
   239→    formatProjectList(context.workspaceSummary.projects),
   240→    "",
   241→    "Recent decisions:",
   242→    formatDecisionList(context.workspaceSummary.recentDecisions),
   243→    "",
   244→    "Open questions:",
   245→    formatQuestionList(context.workspaceSummary.openQuestions),
   246→    "",
   247→    "## Active Observations",
   248→    "Observations are cross-cutting signals from agents about risks, conflicts, and notable facts.",
   249→    "Factor open observations into your reasoning and responses.",
   250→    formatObservationList(context.workspaceSummary.openObservations),
   251→    "",
   252→    "## UI Components",
   253→    "Render structured component blocks when displaying work items or entity extractions.",
   254→    chatComponentSystemPrompt,
   255→    "",
   256→    "### Rendering PM Suggestions",
   257→    "When invoke_pm_agent returns suggestions, render them as a WorkItemSuggestionList:",
   258→    '```component WorkItemSuggestionList { "title": "Suggested Work Items", "items": [{"kind": "task", "title": "...", "rationale": "..."}] }```',
   259→    "Include all fields from the PM result: kind, title, rationale, project, priority, category.",
   260→    "If a suggestion has a possible_duplicate, include possibleDuplicateId, possibleDuplicateName, possibleDuplicateSimilarity.",
   261→    "",
   262→    "### Rendering Relationship Graphs",
   263→    "When show_relationship_graph returns data, render its result directly as an InlineRelationshipGraph component block.",
   264→    "The tool result contains component and props fields -- pass the props as-is to the component.",
   265→    '```component InlineRelationshipGraph { "title": "Relationships: ...", "nodes": [...], "edges": [...], "focusNodeIds": [...] }```',
   266→    "",
   267→  );
   268→
   269→  return sections.join("\n");
   270→}
   271→

     1→import { RecordId } from "surrealdb";
     2→import type { ExtractedEntity, ExtractedRelationship, OnboardingAction } from "../../shared/contracts";
     3→import { loadAssistantConversationContext } from "../extraction/context-loaders";
     4→import { loadBranchChain, loadMessagesWithInheritance } from "./branch-chain";
     5→import { ingestAttachment } from "../extraction/document-ingestion";
     6→import { persistEmbeddings } from "../extraction/embedding-writeback";
     7→import { appendExtractedTools } from "../extraction/persist-extraction";
     8→import type { ConversationRow, GraphEntityRecord, IncomingAttachment, WorkspaceRow } from "../extraction/types";
     9→import { elapsedMs, logError, logInfo, userFacingError } from "../http/observability";
    10→import { transitionOnboardingState } from "../onboarding/onboarding-state";
    11→import type { ServerDependencies } from "../runtime/types";
    12→import { runOrchestrator } from "./handler";
    13→import { getWorkspaceOwnerRecord } from "../graph/queries";
    14→import { refreshConversationTouchedBy, maybeUpgradeConversationTitle } from "../workspace/conversation-sidebar";
    15→import { loadWorkspaceProjects } from "../workspace/workspace-scope";
    16→
    17→export async function processChatMessage(input: {
    18→  deps: ServerDependencies;
    19→  conversationId: string;
    20→  messageId: string;
    21→  workspaceRecord: RecordId<"workspace", string>;
    22→  userMessageRecord: RecordId<"message", string>;
    23→  userText: string;
    24→  attachment?: IncomingAttachment;
    25→  onboardingAction?: OnboardingAction;
    26→}): Promise<void> {
    27→  const startedAt = performance.now();
    28→  logInfo("chat.message.process.execution.started", "Chat message processing execution started", {
    29→    conversationId: input.conversationId,
    30→    messageId: input.messageId,
    31→    workspaceId: input.workspaceRecord.id as string,
    32→    hasAttachment: input.attachment !== undefined,
    33→  });
    34→
    35→  try {
    36→    const now = new Date();
    37→    const conversationRecord = new RecordId("conversation", input.conversationId);
    38→    const workspace = await input.deps.surreal.select<WorkspaceRow>(input.workspaceRecord);
    39→    if (!workspace) {
    40→      throw new Error("workspace not found");
    41→    }
    42→
    43→    const conversation = await input.deps.surreal.select<ConversationRow>(conversationRecord);
    44→    if (!conversation) {
    45→      throw new Error("conversation not found");
    46→    }
    47→
    48→    // Check if this conversation is a branch and load inherited context
    49→    const branchChain = await loadBranchChain(input.deps.surreal, input.conversationId);
    50→    const isBranch = branchChain.length > 0;
    51→
    52→    let inheritedEntityIds: RecordId[] | undefined;
    53→    let assistantContextRows: Array<{ id: RecordId<"message", string>; role: "user" | "assistant"; text: string; createdAt: Date | string }>;
    54→
    55→    if (isBranch) {
    56→      // For branches, load full message history including inherited messages
    57→      const allMessages = await loadMessagesWithInheritance(input.deps.surreal, input.conversationId, 30);
    58→      assistantContextRows = allMessages
    59→        .map((m) => ({
    60→          id: new RecordId("message", m.id),
    61→          role: m.role,
    62→          text: m.text,
    63→          createdAt: m.createdAt,
    64→        }))
    65→        .slice(-10);
    66→
    67→      // Derive inherited entity IDs from extraction_relations on inherited messages
    68→      const inheritedMsgIds = allMessages
    69→        .filter((m) => m.inherited)
    70→        .map((m) => new RecordId("message", m.id));
    71→
    72→      if (inheritedMsgIds.length > 0) {
    73→        const [entityRows] = await input.deps.surreal
    74→          .query<[Array<{ out: RecordId }>]>(
    75→            "SELECT DISTINCT out FROM extraction_relation WHERE `in` IN $msgIds LIMIT 30;",
    76→            { msgIds: inheritedMsgIds },
    77→          )
    78→          .collect<[Array<{ out: RecordId }>]>();
    79→        inheritedEntityIds = entityRows.map((r) => r.out);
    80→      }
    81→    } else {
    82→      assistantContextRows = await loadAssistantConversationContext(input.deps.surreal, input.conversationId);
    83→    }
    84→
    85→    const workspaceProjects = await loadWorkspaceProjects(input.deps.surreal, input.workspaceRecord);
    86→    const workspaceProjectNames = workspaceProjects.map((project) => project.name);
    87→    const persistedEntities: ExtractedEntity[] = [];
    88→    const persistedRelationships: ExtractedRelationship[] = [];
    89→    const embeddingTargets: Array<{ record: GraphEntityRecord; text: string }> = [];
    90→    const extractedTools: string[] = [];
    91→    const unresolvedAssigneeNames = new Set<string>();
    92→
    93→    // Attachment extraction stays automatic — document ingestion is intentional
    94→    if (input.attachment) {
    95→      const ingestion = await ingestAttachment({
    96→        surreal: input.deps.surreal,
    97→        extractionModel: input.deps.extractionModel,
    98→        embeddingModel: input.deps.embeddingModel,
    99→        embeddingDimension: input.deps.config.embeddingDimension,
   100→        extractionStoreThreshold: input.deps.config.extractionStoreThreshold,
   101→        extractionModelId: input.deps.config.extractionModelId,
   102→        workspaceRecord: input.workspaceRecord,
   103→        conversationRecord,
   104→        userMessageRecord: input.userMessageRecord,
   105→        attachment: input.attachment,
   106→        workspaceName: workspace.name,
   107→        projectNames: workspaceProjectNames,
   108→        now,
   109→        onChunkResult: (chunkResult) => {
   110→          if (chunkResult.entities.length > 0 || chunkResult.relationships.length > 0) {
   111→            input.deps.sse.emitEvent(input.messageId, {
   112→              type: "extraction",
   113→              messageId: input.messageId,
   114→              entities: chunkResult.entities,
   115→              relationships: chunkResult.relationships,
   116→            });
   117→          }
   118→        },
   119→      });
   120→
   121→      persistedEntities.push(...ingestion.entities);
   122→      persistedRelationships.push(...ingestion.relationships);
   123→      embeddingTargets.push(...ingestion.embeddingTargets);
   124→      extractedTools.push(...ingestion.tools);
   125→      for (const unresolvedName of ingestion.unresolvedAssigneeNames) {
   126→        unresolvedAssigneeNames.add(unresolvedName);
   127→      }
   128→    }
   129→
   130→    if (extractedTools.length > 0) {
   131→      await appendExtractedTools(input.deps.surreal, input.workspaceRecord, extractedTools, now);
   132→    }
   133→
   134→    const onboardingBefore = workspace.onboarding_complete
   135→      ? "complete"
   136→      : workspace.onboarding_summary_pending
   137→        ? "summary_pending"
   138→        : "active";
   139→
   140→    const onboardingAfter = await transitionOnboardingState({
   141→      surreal: input.deps.surreal,
   142→      workspaceRecord: input.workspaceRecord,
   143→      workspace,
   144→      onboardingAction: input.onboardingAction,
   145→      now,
   146→    });
   147→
   148→    // Always run orchestrator — it handles both onboarding and post-onboarding
   149→    const workspaceOwnerRecord = await getWorkspaceOwnerRecord({
   150→      surreal: input.deps.surreal,
   151→      workspaceRecord: input.workspaceRecord,
   152→    });
   153→
   154→    const graphAwareResponse = await runOrchestrator({
   155→      surreal: input.deps.surreal,
   156→      model: input.deps.assistantModel,
   157→      pmModel: input.deps.pmModel,
   158→      embeddingModel: input.deps.embeddingModel,
   159→      embeddingDimension: input.deps.config.embeddingDimension,
   160→      extractionModelId: input.deps.config.extractionModelId,
   161→      extractionModel: input.deps.extractionModel,
   162→      extractionStoreThreshold: input.deps.config.extractionStoreThreshold,
   163→      conversationRecord,
   164→      workspaceRecord: input.workspaceRecord,
   165→      currentMessageRecord: input.userMessageRecord,
   166→      latestUserText: input.userText,
   167→      isOnboarding: onboardingAfter !== "complete",
   168→      onboardingState: onboardingAfter,
   169→      ...(workspaceOwnerRecord ? { workspaceOwnerRecord } : {}),
   170→      ...(inheritedEntityIds && inheritedEntityIds.length > 0 ? { inheritedEntityIds } : {}),
   171→      messages: assistantContextRows.map((row) => ({
   172→        role: row.role,
   173→        text: row.text,
   174→      })),
   175→      onToken: async (token) => {
   176→        input.deps.sse.emitEvent(input.messageId, {
   177→          type: "token",
   178→          messageId: input.messageId,
   179→          token,
   180→        });
   181→      },
   182→    });
   183→
   184→    let assistantText = graphAwareResponse.text.trim();
   185→    persistedEntities.push(...graphAwareResponse.collectedEntities);
   186→    persistedRelationships.push(...graphAwareResponse.collectedRelationships);
   187→
   188→    const unresolvedAssigneeSuggestions = [...unresolvedAssigneeNames].map(
   189→      (name) => `You mentioned ${name} - want to add them to workspace people?`,
   190→    );
   191→    const assistantSuggestions = sanitizeAssistantSuggestions(unresolvedAssigneeSuggestions, 3);
   192→
   193→    // Post-response hooks (entities now created during orchestrator execution)
   194→    await refreshConversationTouchedBy(input.deps.surreal, conversationRecord);
   195→    await maybeUpgradeConversationTitle(input.deps.surreal, conversationRecord);
   196→
   197→    if (assistantText.trim().length === 0) {
   198→      assistantText = "I could not generate a response for that request.";
   199→    }
   200→
   201→    const assistantMessageRecord = new RecordId("message", input.messageId);
   202→    await input.deps.surreal.create(assistantMessageRecord).content({
   203→      conversation: conversationRecord,
   204→      role: "assistant",
   205→      text: assistantText,
   206→      ...(assistantSuggestions.length > 0 ? { suggestions: assistantSuggestions } : {}),
   207→      createdAt: now,
   208→    });
   209→
   210→    await input.deps.surreal.update(conversationRecord).merge({
   211→      updatedAt: now,
   212→    });
   213→
   214→    void persistEmbeddings({
   215→      surreal: input.deps.surreal,
   216→      embeddingModel: input.deps.embeddingModel,
   217→      embeddingDimension: input.deps.config.embeddingDimension,
   218→      assistantMessageRecord,
   219→      assistantText,
   220→      entities: embeddingTargets,
   221→    }).catch(() => undefined);
   222→
   223→    input.deps.sse.emitEvent(input.messageId, {
   224→      type: "extraction",
   225→      messageId: input.messageId,
   226→      entities: persistedEntities,
   227→      relationships: persistedRelationships,
   228→    });
   229→
   230→    if (onboardingBefore !== onboardingAfter) {
   231→      input.deps.sse.emitEvent(input.messageId, {
   232→        type: "onboarding_state",
   233→        messageId: input.messageId,
   234→        onboardingState: onboardingAfter,
   235→      });
   236→    }
   237→
   238→    input.deps.sse.emitEvent(input.messageId, {
   239→      type: "assistant_message",
   240→      messageId: input.messageId,
   241→      text: assistantText,
   242→      ...(assistantSuggestions.length > 0 ? { suggestions: assistantSuggestions } : {}),
   243→    });
   244→
   245→    input.deps.sse.emitEvent(input.messageId, {
   246→      type: "done",
   247→      messageId: input.messageId,
   248→    });
   249→
   250→    logInfo("chat.message.process.execution.completed", "Chat message processing execution completed", {
   251→      conversationId: input.conversationId,
   252→      messageId: input.messageId,
   253→      workspaceId: input.workspaceRecord.id as string,
   254→      entityCount: persistedEntities.length,
   255→      relationshipCount: persistedRelationships.length,
   256→      durationMs: elapsedMs(startedAt),
   257→    });
   258→  } catch (error) {
   259→    logError("chat.message.process.execution.failed", "Chat message processing execution failed", error, {
   260→      conversationId: input.conversationId,
   261→      messageId: input.messageId,
   262→      workspaceId: input.workspaceRecord.id as string,
   263→      durationMs: elapsedMs(startedAt),
   264→    });
   265→    const errorText = userFacingError(error, "chat processing failed");
   266→    input.deps.sse.emitEvent(input.messageId, {
   267→      type: "error",
   268→      messageId: input.messageId,
   269→      error: errorText,
   270→    });
   271→  }
   272→}
   273→
   274→function sanitizeAssistantSuggestions(suggestions: string[], limit: number): string[] {
   275→  return [...new Set(suggestions.map((value) => value.trim()))]
   276→    .filter((value) => value.length > 0 && value.length <= 140)
   277→    .slice(0, limit);
   278→}
   279→
```

     1→import { describe, expect, it } from "bun:test";
     2→import { randomUUID } from "node:crypto";
     3→import { RecordId, type Surreal } from "surrealdb";
     4→import { collectSseEvents, fetchJson, setupSmokeSuite } from "./smoke-test-kit";
     5→
     6→type ChatMessageResponse = {
     7→  messageId: string;
     8→  userMessageId: string;
     9→  conversationId: string;
    10→  workspaceId: string;
    11→  streamUrl: string;
    12→};
    13→
    14→type StreamEvent =
    15→  | { type: "assistant_message"; messageId: string; text: string }
    16→  | {
    17→      type: "extraction";
    18→      messageId: string;
    19→      entities: Array<{ id: string; kind: string; text: string; confidence: number }>;
    20→    }
    21→  | { type: "done"; messageId: string }
    22→  | { type: "error"; messageId: string; error: string }
    23→  | { type: string; messageId: string };
    24→
    25→const getRuntime = setupSmokeSuite("pipeline");
    26→
    27→async function createOnboardedWorkspace(
    28→  baseUrl: string,
    29→  surreal: Surreal,
    30→): Promise<{ workspaceId: string; conversationId: string }> {
    31→  const workspace = await fetchJson<{ workspaceId: string; conversationId: string }>(`${baseUrl}/api/workspaces`, {
    32→    method: "POST",
    33→    headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
    34→    body: JSON.stringify({
    35→      name: `Pipeline Smoke ${Date.now()}`,
    36→      ownerDisplayName: "Marcus",
    37→    }),
    38→  });
    39→
    40→  // Fast-forward onboarding so orchestrator runs in post-onboarding mode
    41→  const workspaceRecord = new RecordId("workspace", workspace.workspaceId);
    42→  await surreal.update(workspaceRecord).merge({
    43→    onboarding_complete: true,
    44→    onboarding_summary_pending: false,
    45→    onboarding_completed_at: new Date(),
    46→  });
    47→
    48→  return workspace;
    49→}
    50→
    51→describe("agent-controlled extraction smoke", () => {
    52→  it("orchestrator handles decision language", async () => {

    60→        clientMessageId: randomUUID(),

    63→        text: "We have decided to use TypeScript instead of Rust for the backend implementation. Please record this decision.",
    64→      }),
    65→    });
    66→
    67→    const events = await collectSseEvents<StreamEvent>(`${baseUrl}${message.streamUrl}`, 120_000);
    68→    const assistantEvent = events.find((event) => event.type === "assistant_message");
    69→    expect(assistantEvent).toBeDefined();
    70→    if (!assistantEvent || assistantEvent.type !== "assistant_message") {
    71→      throw new Error("Expected assistant_message event");
    72→    }
    73→
    74→    // The orchestrator should either create a decision entity in DB

   187→  }, 180_000);

   189→
   190→async function countWorkspaceEntities(
   191→  surreal: Surreal,
   192→  workspaceRecord: RecordId<"workspace", string>,
   193→): Promise<number> {
   194→  const [taskRows] = await surreal
   195→    .query<[Array<{ id: RecordId }>]>("SELECT id FROM task WHERE workspace = $workspace;", {
   196→      workspace: workspaceRecord,
   197→    })
   198→    .collect<[Array<{ id: RecordId }>]>();
   199→
   200→  const [decisionRows] = await surreal
   201→    .query<[Array<{ id: RecordId }>]>("SELECT id FROM decision WHERE workspace = $workspace;", {
   202→      workspace: workspaceRecord,
   203→    })
   204→    .collect<[Array<{ id: RecordId }>]>();
   205→
   206→  const [questionRows] = await surreal
   207→    .query<[Array<{ id: RecordId }>]>(
   208→      "SELECT id FROM question WHERE workspace = $workspace;",
   209→      { workspace: workspaceRecord },
   210→    )
   211→    .collect<[Array<{ id: RecordId }>]>();
   212→
   213→  const [projectRows] = await surreal
   214→    .query<[Array<{ id: RecordId }>]>(
   215→      "SELECT id FROM project WHERE id IN (SELECT VALUE out FROM has_project WHERE `in` = $workspace);",
   216→      { workspace: workspaceRecord },
   217→    )
   218→    .collect<[Array<{ id: RecordId }>]>();
   219→
   220→  return taskRows.length + decisionRows.length + questionRows.length + projectRows.length;
   221→}

     1→import { describe, expect, it } from "bun:test";

     9→
    10→describe("observation queries", () => {
    11→  it("creates an observation and relates it to a target entity", async () => {
    12→    const createdPayloads: unknown[] = [];
    13→    const relateCalls: Array<{ in: RecordId<"observation", string>; out: RecordId<string, string>; edge: RecordId<"observes", string>; payload: unknown }> = [];
    14→
    15→    const surrealMock = {
    16→      create: (_record: RecordId<"observation", string>) => ({
    17→        content: async (payload: unknown) => {
    18→          createdPayloads.push(payload);
    19→        },
    20→      }),
    21→      relate: (
    22→        inRecord: RecordId<"observation", string>,
    23→        edgeRecord: RecordId<"observes", string>,
    24→        outRecord: RecordId<string, string>,
    25→        payload: unknown,
    26→      ) => {
    27→        relateCalls.push({
    28→          in: inRecord,
    29→          edge: edgeRecord,
    30→          out: outRecord,
    31→          payload,
    32→        });
    33→        return {
    34→          output: async () => ({ id: edgeRecord }),
    35→        };
    36→      },
    37→    };
    38→
    39→    const workspaceRecord = new RecordId("workspace", "w-1");
    40→    const sourceMessageRecord = new RecordId("message", "m-1");
    41→    const taskRecord = new RecordId("task", "t-1");
    42→
    43→    const observationRecord = await createObservation({
    44→      surreal: surrealMock as any,
    45→      workspaceRecord,
    46→      text: "Auth implementation blocks launch.",
    47→      severity: "warning",
    48→      category: "engineering",
    49→      sourceAgent: "orchestrator",
    50→      now: new Date("2026-01-15T10:00:00.000Z"),
    51→      sourceMessageRecord,
    52→      relatedRecord: taskRecord,
    53→      embedding: [0.1, 0.2, 0.3],
    54→    });
    55→
    56→    expect(observationRecord.table.name).toBe("observation");
    57→    expect(createdPayloads).toHaveLength(1);
    58→    expect(createdPayloads[0]).toMatchObject({
    59→      text: "Auth implementation blocks launch.",
    60→      severity: "warning",
    61→      category: "engineering",
    62→      status: "open",
    63→      source_agent: "orchestrator",
    64→      workspace: workspaceRecord,
    65→      source_message: sourceMessageRecord,
    66→      embedding: [0.1, 0.2, 0.3],

    68→
    69→    expect(relateCalls).toHaveLength(1);
    70→    expect(relateCalls[0]?.in.table.name).toBe("observation");
    71→    expect(relateCalls[0]?.edge.table.name).toBe("observes");
    72→    expect(relateCalls[0]?.out).toBe(taskRecord);
    73→  });
    74→
    75→  it("fails acknowledging an out-of-scope observation", async () => {
    76→    const surrealMock = {
    77→      select: async () => ({ workspace: new RecordId("workspace", "w-other") }),
    78→      update: () => ({

    88→        now: new Date(),
    89→      }),

    92→
    93→  it("resolves a scoped observation", async () => {
    94→    const mergePayloads: unknown[] = [];

    98→    const surrealMock = {

   107→    await resolveObservation({

```
     1→import { generateText, Output, stepCountIs } from "ai";
     2→import { z } from "zod";
     3→import { ENTITY_CATEGORIES, ENTITY_PRIORITIES, type ExtractedEntity, type ExtractedRelationship } from "../../../shared/contracts";
     4→import type { ChatToolDeps, ChatToolExecutionContext } from "../../chat/tools/types";
     5→import { buildPmSystemPrompt } from "./prompt";
     6→import { createPmTools } from "./tools";
     7→
     8→const workItemSuggestionSchema = z.object({
     9→  kind: z.enum(["task", "feature", "project"]),
    10→  title: z.string().min(1),
    11→  rationale: z.string().min(1),
    12→  category: z.enum(ENTITY_CATEGORIES).optional(),
    13→  project: z.string().optional(),
    14→  priority: z.enum(ENTITY_PRIORITIES).optional(),
    15→  possible_duplicate: z
    16→    .object({
    17→      id: z.string().min(1),
    18→      name: z.string().min(1),
    19→      similarity: z.number().min(0).max(1),
    20→    })
    21→    .optional(),
    22→});
    23→
    24→const pmAgentResultSchema = z.object({
    25→  summary: z.string().min(1),
    26→  suggestions: z.array(workItemSuggestionSchema),
    27→  updated: z.array(
    28→    z.object({
    29→      existing_id: z.string().min(1),
    30→      title: z.string().min(1),
    31→      changes: z.string().min(1),
    32→    }),
    33→  ),
    34→  discarded: z.array(
    35→    z.object({
    36→      title: z.string().min(1),
    37→      reason: z.string().min(1),
    38→    }),
    39→  ),
    40→  observations_created: z.array(z.string().min(1)),
    41→});
    42→
    43→export type WorkItemSuggestion = z.infer<typeof workItemSuggestionSchema>;
    44→export type PmAgentResult = z.infer<typeof pmAgentResultSchema>;
    45→
    46→export type PmAgentOutput = PmAgentResult & {
    47→  extracted_entities: ExtractedEntity[];
    48→  extracted_relationships: ExtractedRelationship[];
    49→};
    50→
    51→export type PmAgentInput = {
    52→  deps: ChatToolDeps & { pmModel: any };
    53→  context: ChatToolExecutionContext;
    54→  intent: "plan_work" | "check_status" | "organize" | "track_dependencies";
    55→  conversationContext: string;
    56→  project?: string;
    57→};
    58→
    59→const INTENT_INSTRUCTIONS: Record<PmAgentInput["intent"], string> = {
    60→  check_status: "Primary action: call get_project_status when a project scope is available.",
    61→  plan_work: "Primary action: propose tasks/features/projects with suggest_work_items or create_work_item, deduping each item.",
    62→  track_dependencies: "Primary action: identify blockers/dependencies and create observations for high-risk paths.",
    63→  organize: "Primary action: organize work into clear, deduplicated next steps.",
    64→};
    65→
    66→export async function runPmAgent(input: PmAgentInput): Promise<PmAgentOutput> {
    67→  const system = await buildPmSystemPrompt({
    68→    surreal: input.deps.surreal,
    69→    workspaceRecord: input.context.workspaceRecord,
    70→  });
    71→
    72→  const result = await generateText({
    73→    model: input.deps.pmModel,
    74→    system,
    75→    prompt: [
    76→      "You are handling a PM request.",
    77→      `Intent: ${input.intent}`,
    78→      input.project ? `Project hint: ${input.project}` : "Project hint: not provided",
    79→      INTENT_INSTRUCTIONS[input.intent],
    80→      "Context:",
    81→      input.conversationContext,
    82→    ].join("\n"),
    83→    tools: createPmTools(input.deps),
    84→    output: Output.object({ schema: pmAgentResultSchema }),
    85→    experimental_context: input.context,
    86→    stopWhen: stepCountIs(6),
    87→  });
    88→
    89→  if (!result.output) {
    90→    throw new Error("pm agent did not produce structured output");
    91→  }
    92→
    93→  const extracted_entities: ExtractedEntity[] = [];
    94→  const extracted_relationships: ExtractedRelationship[] = [];
    95→
    96→  for (const step of result.steps) {
    97→    for (const toolResult of step.toolResults) {
    98→      const res = toolResult.output as Record<string, unknown> | undefined;
    99→      if (res) {
   100→        if (Array.isArray(res.extracted_entities)) {
   101→          extracted_entities.push(...(res.extracted_entities as ExtractedEntity[]));
   102→        }
   103→        if (Array.isArray(res.extracted_relationships)) {
   104→          extracted_relationships.push(...(res.extracted_relationships as ExtractedRelationship[]));
   105→        }
   106→      }
   107→    }
   108→  }
   109→
   110→  return {
   111→    ...result.output,
   112→    extracted_entities,
   113→    extracted_relationships,
   114→  };
   115→}
   116→
```

```
     1→import { tool } from "ai";
     2→import { z } from "zod";
     3→import { confirmDecisionRecord, getDecisionRecordForWorkspace, getWorkspaceOwnerRecord } from "../../graph/queries";
     4→import { requireToolContext, toDecisionRecordId } from "./helpers";
     5→import type { ChatToolDeps } from "./types";
     6→
     7→const CONFIRMABLE_STATUSES = new Set(["provisional", "inferred"]);
     8→
     9→export function createConfirmDecisionTool(deps: ChatToolDeps) {
    10→  return tool({
    11→    description:
    12→      "Confirm a provisional or inferred decision. ONLY use this when the user explicitly approves a decision in conversation. Never call this without clear user authorization.",
    13→    inputSchema: z.object({
    14→      decision_id: z.string().min(1).describe("Decision record ID to confirm"),
    15→      notes: z.string().optional().describe("Additional user context"),
    16→    }),
    17→    execute: async (input, options) => {
    18→      const context = requireToolContext(options);
    19→
    20→      if (context.actor !== "chat_agent" && context.actor !== "orchestrator") {
    21→        throw new Error("confirm_decision is only available for chat_agent or orchestrator context");
    22→      }
    23→
    24→      const decisionRecord = toDecisionRecordId(input.decision_id);
    25→      const decision = await getDecisionRecordForWorkspace({
    26→        surreal: deps.surreal,
    27→        workspaceRecord: context.workspaceRecord,
    28→        decisionInput: `decision:${decisionRecord.id as string}`,
    29→      });
    30→
    31→      if (!CONFIRMABLE_STATUSES.has(decision.status)) {
    32→        throw new Error(`decision is not confirmable from status '${decision.status}'`);
    33→      }
    34→
    35→      const ownerRecord = context.workspaceOwnerRecord
    36→        ? context.workspaceOwnerRecord
    37→        : await getWorkspaceOwnerRecord({
    38→            surreal: deps.surreal,
    39→            workspaceRecord: context.workspaceRecord,
    40→          });
    41→
    42→      const confirmedAt = new Date();
    43→      await confirmDecisionRecord({
    44→        surreal: deps.surreal,
    45→        decisionRecord: decision.id,
    46→        confirmedAt,
    47→        ...(ownerRecord ? { confirmedBy: ownerRecord } : {}),
    48→        ...(input.notes ? { notes: input.notes } : {}),
    49→      });
    50→
    51→      return {
    52→        decision_id: `decision:${decision.id.id as string}`,
    53→        status: "confirmed",
    54→        name: decision.summary,
    55→      };
    56→    },
    57→  });
    58→}
    59→
```

     6→
     7→export function createInvokePmAgentTool(deps: OrchestratorToolDeps) {
     8→  return tool({
     9→    description:
    10→      "Invoke the PM subagent — the single authority on tasks, features, and project status.",
    11→    inputSchema: z.object({
    12→      intent: z
    13→        .enum(["plan_work", "check_status", "organize", "track_dependencies"])
    14→        .describe(
    15→          "plan_work: user discusses goals, features, or work to be done. check_status: user asks about progress, blockers, or project status. organize: user wants to restructure or re-prioritize. track_dependencies: user asks about blocked items or dependency chains.",
    16→        ),
    17→      context: z.string().min(1).describe("Conversation context for the PM agent"),
    18→      project: z.string().optional().describe("Optional project scope"),
    19→    }),

    25→        context,
    26→        intent: input.intent,
    27→        conversationContext: input.context,
    28→        ...(input.project ? { project: input.project } : {}),
    29→      });
    30→    },
    31→  });
    32→}
    33→

```
    78→  - `errors.ts` + `observability.ts` (error/log primitives)
    79→- SSE state management is isolated in `app/src/server/streaming/sse-registry.ts`.
    80→- Route/business domains are separated by workflow:
    81→  - `workspace/*` for workspace create/bootstrap/scope checks
    82→  - `chat/*` for ingress, orchestrator, async message processing
    83→  - `entities/*` for entity search, detail, actions, and work item accept endpoints
    84→  - `onboarding/*` for onboarding state and guided replies
    85→  - `extraction/*` for extraction generation, persistence, dedupe/upsert, embeddings, and context loaders
    86→  - `agents/*` for specialized subagent implementations (PM agent)
    87→  - `observation/*` for observation CRUD queries
    88→- `graph/*` contains reusable Surreal graph queries used by chat/tools and higher-level workflows.
    89→
    90→### Orchestrator Agent Architecture
    91→
    92→The chat system uses a thin orchestrator pattern where a single top-level agent dispatches to specialized subagents. The knowledge graph is the communication bus — agents read from and write to the graph independently, never passing data directly between each other.
    93→
    94→```
    95→User Message
    96→  │
    97→  ├─→ Extraction Pipeline (always runs, Haiku)
    98→  │     └─→ entities/relationships → SurrealDB graph
    99→  │
   100→  └─→ Orchestrator (Sonnet, thin)
   101→        ├─→ Direct tools (search, entity detail, decisions, observations)
   102→        └─→ Subagent dispatch
   103→              └─→ PM Agent (Haiku) → suggestions, observations → graph
   104→```
   105→
   106→**Two paths to the graph:**
   107→| Source | Path | Why |
   108→|--------|------|-----|
   109→| User messages | Extraction pipeline (Haiku infers entities from unstructured text) | User input is unstructured |
   110→| Agent output | Direct graph write (agents already have structured form) | Nothing to extract |
   111→
   112→**Key files:**
   113→- `chat/handler.ts` — `runOrchestrator()`: streams orchestrator responses with tool use
   114→- `chat/context.ts` — `buildChatContext()` / `buildSystemPrompt()`: loads graph context, builds orchestrator system prompt
   115→- `chat/tools/index.ts` — `createOrchestratorTools()`: registers all 12 orchestrator tools
   116→- `chat/tools/types.ts` — `ChatToolExecutionContext`: actor-typed context (`chat_agent | mcp | orchestrator | pm_agent`)
   117→
   118→### Orchestrator Tools
   119→
   120→| Tool | Purpose |
   121→|------|---------|
   122→| `search_entities` | Search workspace entities by text query |
   123→| `get_entity_detail` | Fetch entity with relationships and provenance |
   124→| `get_project_status` | Project task/decision/question aggregation |
   125→| `get_conversation_history` | Load recent conversation messages |
   126→| `create_provisional_decision` | Draft a decision for user review |
   127→| `confirm_decision` | Finalize a decision (requires explicit user auth) |
   128→| `resolve_decision` | Mark a decision as resolved |
   129→| `check_constraints` | Validate decision constraints |
   130→| `create_observation` | Create observation for risks/conflicts/signals |
   131→| `acknowledge_observation` | Mark observation as reviewed |
   132→| `resolve_observation` | Close a resolved observation |
   133→| `invoke_pm_agent` | Delegate to PM subagent |
   134→
   135→### Product Manager Subagent
   136→
   137→The PM agent (`agents/pm/`) is the single authority on tasks, features, and project status. It is invoked by the orchestrator via `invoke_pm_agent` tool with an intent:
   138→
   139→| Intent | When to use |
   140→|--------|-------------|
   141→| `plan_work` | User discusses goals, features, or work to be done |
   142→| `check_status` | User asks about project status, progress, or blockers |
   143→| `organize` | User wants to restructure or re-prioritize |
   144→| `track_dependencies` | User asks about blocked items or dependency chains |
   145→
   146→**Key files:**
   147→- `agents/pm/agent.ts` — `runPmAgent()`: generates text with PM tools, parses strict JSON output
   148→- `agents/pm/prompt.ts` — `buildPmSystemPrompt()`: loads workspace projects and observations
   149→- `agents/pm/tools.ts` — `createPmTools()`: search_entities, get_project_status, create_observation, suggest_work_items
   150→- `agents/pm/suggest-work-items.ts` — Embedding-based semantic dedup (>0.97 exact duplicate, ≥0.8 merge, <0.8 new)
   151→
   152→**PM output schema:** `{ summary, suggestions: WorkItemSuggestion[], updated, discarded, observations_created }`
   153→
   154→The orchestrator renders PM suggestions as `WorkItemSuggestionList` component blocks in the chat UI.
   155→
   156→### Observation Entity
   157→
   158→Observations (`observation/*`) are lightweight cross-cutting signals that agents write to the graph. They enable async agent-to-agent communication without forcing signals into wrong entity types.
   159→
   160→- **Severity levels:** `conflict` (contradictions needing human resolution), `warning` (risks), `info` (awareness)
   161→- **Lifecycle:** `open` → `acknowledged` → `resolved`
   162→- **Schema:** `observation` table with text, severity, status, category, source_agent, workspace, embedding
   163→- **Relation:** `observes` edge links observations to project/feature/task/decision/question
   164→- Agents load open observations as part of their context and factor them into their work.
   165→
   166→### Work Item Accept Flow
   167→
   168→When the PM agent suggests work items, the orchestrator renders them as `WorkItemSuggestionList` components. Users can accept or dismiss each item:
   169→
   170→- Accept calls `POST /api/workspaces/:workspaceId/work-items/accept`
   171→- The endpoint creates a `task` or `feature` record in SurrealDB with embedding and optional project linking
   172→- Implemented in `entities/work-item-accept-route.ts`
   173→
   174→### Primary Chat Flow (`POST /api/chat/messages`)
   175→
   176→- `chat/chat-ingress.ts` validates/persists user input and registers an SSE stream message id.
   177→- `chat/chat-processor.ts` orchestrates async processing:
```

   174→### Primary Chat Flow (`POST /api/chat/messages`)
   175→
   176→- `chat/chat-ingress.ts` validates/persists user input and registers an SSE stream message id.
   177→- `chat/chat-processor.ts` orchestrates async processing:
   178→  - load conversation + graph context
   179→  - run extraction (message and optional attachment chunks)
   180→  - persist entities/relationships/provenance
   181→  - transition onboarding state
   182→  - generate assistant response (onboarding reply or orchestrator with subagent dispatch)
   183→  - emit SSE events (`token`, `extraction`, `onboarding_seed`, `onboarding_state`, `observation`, `assistant_message`, `done|error`)
   184→
   185→### RecordId and Table Access Rules
   186→
   187→- Use `RecordId` objects everywhere for Surreal identifiers (never `table:id` strings).
   188→- Server extraction types define typed record aliases:
   189→  - `GraphEntityRecord` and `SourceRecord` are `RecordId<UnionOfTables, string>` aliases.
   190→- Use `record.table.name` for table branching (the SDK's public API; `.tb` is an undeclared internal field that may break on upgrade).

Clean. Here's a summary of what was renamed across **12 files**:

**Code identifiers:**
- `runOrchestrator` → `runChatAgent` (handler.ts, chat-processor.ts)
- `OrchestratorResult` → `ChatAgentResult` (handler.ts)
- `createOrchestratorTools` → `createChatAgentTools` (tools/index.ts, handler.ts)
- `OrchestratorToolDeps` → `ChatAgentToolDeps` (tools/types.ts, tools/index.ts, tools/invoke-pm-agent.ts)

**Actor enum:**
- Removed `"orchestrator"` from the `actor` union type — collapsed into `"chat_agent"`
- Updated `actor: "orchestrator"` → `actor: "chat_agent"` in handler.ts
- Simplified confirm-decision guard to only check `"chat_agent"`

**System prompt:**
- "You are the Orchestrator agent" → "You are the Chat agent"

**Tests & docs:**
- Updated all test descriptions, comments, and test data (`source_agent`, assertion strings)
- Updated AGENTS.md throughout (architecture section, tools section, key files, PM agent docs, chat flow)

The term "thin orchestrator" is kept as an architectural pattern description (in the system prompt and AGENTS.md) since that's what the pattern _is_ — but the agent itself is now called "chat agent" everywhere.

```
# Subagents in the AI SDK: Architecture and Patterns

## Core Concept

Subagents enable delegated, autonomous execution. A parent agent invokes a subagent via a tool, which executes independently with its own context window before returning a result. As the documentation states: "A subagent is an agent that a parent agent can invoke."

## Architecture: Tool-Based Delegation

The AI SDK implements subagents through a straightforward tool pattern:

1. **Define the subagent** with its own model, instructions, and tools
2. **Create a tool** that the parent agent can call
3. **Subagent executes independently** with isolated context
4. **Return results** optionally with UI streaming

## When to Use Subagents

The SDK recommends subagents for specific scenarios:

| Scenario | Rationale |
|----------|-----------|
| Large token exploration | "Tasks require exploring large amounts of tokens" |
| Parallel research | "You need to parallelize independent research" |
| Context overflow | Context would exceed model limits |
| Tool isolation | Different agents need different capabilities |

Conversely, avoid them for simple, focused tasks where sequential processing suffices.

## Key Patterns

### Pattern 1: Basic Subagent (No Streaming)

The simplest implementation blocks until completion:

```ts
const researchTool = tool({
  description: 'Research a topic or question in depth.',
  inputSchema: z.object({
    task: z.string().describe('The research task to complete'),
  }),
  execute: async ({ task }, { abortSignal }) => {
    const result = await researchSubagent.generate({
      prompt: task,
      abortSignal,
    });
    return result.text;
  },
});
```

This pattern works when UI progress visibility isn't needed. The tool call blocks until the subagent completes.

### Pattern 2: Streaming Progress

For real-time UI updates, use async generators with `readUIMessageStream`:

```ts
execute: async function* ({ task }, { abortSignal }) {
  const result = await researchSubagent.stream({
    prompt: task,
    abortSignal,
  });

  for await (const message of readUIMessageStream({
    stream: result.toUIMessageStream(),
  })) {
    yield message;
  }
},
```

Each yield sends a complete, accumulated message to the frontend, displaying incremental progress.

### Pattern 3: Context Management with `toModelOutput`

This is the most powerful pattern for maintaining main agent coherence:

```ts
toModelOutput: ({ output: message }) => {
  const lastTextPart = message?.parts.findLast(p => p.type === 'text');
  return {
    type: 'text',
    value: lastTextPart?.text ?? 'Task completed.',
  };
},
```

**Key insight**: The subagent can use hundreds of thousands of tokens, but `toModelOutput` controls what the model actually sees—typically just the final summary. Users see full execution; the model sees only the condensed result.

## Benefits and Trade-offs

**Advantages:**
- Offload context-heavy exploration to isolated agents
- Parallelize independent research tasks
- Implement specialized orchestration architectures
- Keep main agent context clean and focused

**Costs:**
- Added latency from multiple agent invocations
- Increased complexity in implementation
- Isolated context means no automatic access to main agent history

## Important Caveats

1. **No tool approvals**: Subagent tools cannot use `needsApproval`—all execution is automatic
2. **Isolated context**: Each invocation starts fresh; passing conversation history defeats isolation benefits
3. **Streaming complexity**: Basic patterns are easier to debug; only add streaming when UI progress is necessary

## Rendering in the UI

Tool parts have state indicators for detecting progress:

```
'input-streaming' → 'input-available' → 'output-available'
```

Check `part.preliminary === true` to detect streaming versus complete output. Export agent message types for type safety in React components using `InferAgentUIMessage`.
```

     1→import { generateText, Output, stepCountIs } from "ai";
     2→import { z } from "zod";
     3→import { ENTITY_CATEGORIES, ENTITY_PRIORITIES, type ExtractedEntity, type ExtractedRelationship } from "../../../shared/contracts";
     4→import type { ChatToolDeps, ChatToolExecutionContext } from "../../chat/tools/types";
     5→import { buildPmSystemPrompt } from "./prompt";
     6→import { createPmTools } from "./tools";
     7→
     8→const workItemSuggestionSchema = z.object({
     9→  kind: z.enum(["task", "feature", "project"]),
    10→  title: z.string().min(1),
    11→  rationale: z.string().min(1),
    12→  category: z.enum(ENTITY_CATEGORIES).optional(),
    13→  project: z.string().optional(),
    14→  priority: z.enum(ENTITY_PRIORITIES).optional(),
    15→  possible_duplicate: z
    16→    .object({
    17→      id: z.string().min(1),
    18→      name: z.string().min(1),
    19→      similarity: z.number().min(0).max(1),
    20→    })
    21→    .optional(),
    22→});
    23→
    24→const pmAgentResultSchema = z.object({
    25→  summary: z.string().min(1),
    26→  suggestions: z.array(workItemSuggestionSchema),
    27→  updated: z.array(
    28→    z.object({
    29→      existing_id: z.string().min(1),
    30→      title: z.string().min(1),
    31→      changes: z.string().min(1),
    32→    }),
    33→  ),
    34→  discarded: z.array(
    35→    z.object({
    36→      title: z.string().min(1),
    37→      reason: z.string().min(1),
    38→    }),
    39→  ),
    40→  observations_created: z.array(z.string().min(1)),
    41→});
    42→
    43→export type WorkItemSuggestion = z.infer<typeof workItemSuggestionSchema>;
    44→export type PmAgentResult = z.infer<typeof pmAgentResultSchema>;
    45→
    46→export type PmAgentOutput = PmAgentResult & {
    47→  extracted_entities: ExtractedEntity[];
    48→  extracted_relationships: ExtractedRelationship[];
    49→};
    50→
    51→export type PmAgentInput = {
    52→  deps: ChatToolDeps & { pmModel: any };
    53→  context: ChatToolExecutionContext;
    54→  intent: "plan_work" | "check_status" | "organize" | "track_dependencies";
    55→  conversationContext: string;
    56→  project?: string;
    57→};
    58→
    59→const INTENT_INSTRUCTIONS: Record<PmAgentInput["intent"], string> = {
    60→  check_status: "Primary action: call get_project_status when a project scope is available.",
    61→  plan_work: "Primary action: propose tasks/features/projects with suggest_work_items or create_work_item, deduping each item.",
    62→  track_dependencies: "Primary action: identify blockers/dependencies and create observations for high-risk paths.",
    63→  organize: "Primary action: organize work into clear, deduplicated next steps.",
    64→};
    65→
    66→export async function runPmAgent(input: PmAgentInput): Promise<PmAgentOutput> {
    67→  const system = await buildPmSystemPrompt({
    68→    surreal: input.deps.surreal,
    69→    workspaceRecord: input.context.workspaceRecord,
    70→  });
    71→
    72→  const result = await generateText({
    73→    model: input.deps.pmModel,
    74→    system,
    75→    prompt: [
    76→      "You are handling a PM request.",
    77→      `Intent: ${input.intent}`,
    78→      input.project ? `Project hint: ${input.project}` : "Project hint: not provided",
    79→      INTENT_INSTRUCTIONS[input.intent],
    80→      "Context:",
    81→      input.conversationContext,
    82→    ].join("\n"),
    83→    tools: createPmTools(input.deps),
    84→    output: Output.object({ schema: pmAgentResultSchema }),
    85→    experimental_context: input.context,
    86→    stopWhen: stepCountIs(6),
    87→  });
    88→
    89→  if (!result.output) {
    90→    throw new Error("pm agent did not produce structured output");
    91→  }
    92→
    93→  const extracted_entities: ExtractedEntity[] = [];
    94→  const extracted_relationships: ExtractedRelationship[] = [];
    95→
    96→  for (const step of result.steps) {
    97→    for (const toolResult of step.toolResults) {
    98→      const res = toolResult.output as Record<string, unknown> | undefined;
    99→      if (res) {
   100→        if (Array.isArray(res.extracted_entities)) {
   101→          extracted_entities.push(...(res.extracted_entities as ExtractedEntity[]));
   102→        }
   103→        if (Array.isArray(res.extracted_relationships)) {
   104→          extracted_relationships.push(...(res.extracted_relationships as ExtractedRelationship[]));
   105→        }
   106→      }
   107→    }
   108→  }
   109→
   110→  return {
   111→    ...result.output,
   112→    extracted_entities,
   113→    extracted_relationships,
   114→  };
   115→}
   116→
```

    31→  workspaceRecord: RecordId<"workspace", string>;
    32→  currentMessageRecord: RecordId<"message", string>;
    33→  latestUserText: string;
    34→  workspaceOwnerRecord?: RecordId<"person", string>;
    35→  inheritedEntityIds?: RecordId[];
    36→  messages: ConversationMessage[];
    37→  isOnboarding?: boolean;
    38→  onboardingState?: OnboardingState;
    39→  onToken: (token: string) => Promise<void> | void;
    40→}): Promise<ChatAgentResult> {
    41→  const context = await buildChatContext({
    42→    surreal: input.surreal,
    43→    conversationRecord: input.conversationRecord,
    44→    workspaceRecord: input.workspaceRecord,
    45→    ...(input.inheritedEntityIds && input.inheritedEntityIds.length > 0
    46→      ? { inheritedEntityIds: input.inheritedEntityIds }
    47→      : {}),
    48→  });
    49→
    50→  const system = buildSystemPrompt(context, {
    51→    isOnboarding: input.isOnboarding,
    52→    onboardingState: input.onboardingState,
    53→  });
    54→  const modelMessages: ModelMessage[] = input.messages.map((message) => ({
    55→    role: message.role,
    56→    content: message.text,
    57→  }));
    58→
    59→  const result = streamText({
    60→    model: input.model,
    61→    system,
    62→    messages: modelMessages,
    63→    tools: createChatAgentTools({
    64→      surreal: input.surreal,
    65→      pmModel: input.pmModel,
    66→      embeddingModel: input.embeddingModel,
    67→      embeddingDimension: input.embeddingDimension,
    68→      extractionModelId: input.extractionModelId,
    69→      extractionModel: input.extractionModel,
    70→      extractionStoreThreshold: input.extractionStoreThreshold,
    71→    }),
    72→    experimental_context: {
    73→      actor: "chat_agent",
    74→      workspaceRecord: input.workspaceRecord,
    75→      conversationRecord: input.conversationRecord,
    76→      currentMessageRecord: input.currentMessageRecord,
    77→      latestUserText: input.latestUserText,
    78→      ...(input.workspaceOwnerRecord ? { workspaceOwnerRecord: input.workspaceOwnerRecord } : {}),
    79→    },
    80→    stopWhen: stepCountIs(5),
    81→  });
    82→
    83→  let text = "";
    84→  const collectedEntities: CollectedEntity[] = [];
    85→  const collectedRelationships: CollectedRelationship[] = [];
    86→
    87→  for await (const part of result.fullStream) {
    88→    if (part.type === "text-delta") {
    89→      text = `${text}${part.text}`;
    90→      await input.onToken(part.text);
    91→      continue;
    92→    }
    93→
    94→    if (part.type === "tool-result") {
    95→      const toolResult = part.output as Record<string, unknown> | undefined;
    96→      if (toolResult) {
    97→        if (Array.isArray(toolResult.extracted_entities)) {
    98→          collectedEntities.push(...(toolResult.extracted_entities as CollectedEntity[]));
    99→        }
   100→        if (Array.isArray(toolResult.extracted_relationships)) {
   101→          collectedRelationships.push(...(toolResult.extracted_relationships as CollectedRelationship[]));
   102→        }
   103→      }
   104→    }
   105→  }
   106→
   107→  if (text.trim().length === 0) {
   108→    text = await result.text;
   109→  }
   110→
   111→  return {
   112→    text: text.trim(),
   113→    collectedEntities,
   114→    collectedRelationships,
   115→  };
   116→}
   117→
   118→export const runGraphAwareChat = runChatAgent;

You're right to question this. Here's what's actually happening:

**The PM agent only has 2 unique tools** — `suggest_work_items` and `create_work_item`. The other 3 (`search_entities`, `get_project_status`, `create_observation`) are already in the chat agent's toolset. So the "subagent" is really just a way to:

1. Switch to a cheaper model (Haiku) for PM work
2. Use a different system prompt (`buildPmSystemPrompt`)
3. Force structured JSON output (`Output.object`)
4. Gate behind an intent enum

But the current implementation uses `generateText` (not streaming), blocks the chat agent's stream while it runs, and the chat agent manually collects `extracted_entities` from tool results afterward. It's a hand-rolled version of exactly what the AI SDK subagent pattern does natively.

The SDK's subagent pattern gives you:
- Tool-based invocation (already have this)
- Streaming progress to the UI via `readUIMessageStream` (don't have — currently blocks)
- `toModelOutput` to control what goes back into the parent's context (don't have — currently dumps everything)

So the real question is: **should the PM tools just be flattened into the chat agent?**

**Arguments for flattening:**
- `suggest_work_items` and `create_work_item` are the only unique tools — just add them directly
- Eliminates the `invoke_pm_agent` indirection + intent routing
- The chat agent already has full graph context; the PM agent loads a subset of the same context
- One fewer LLM call per PM interaction

**Arguments for keeping separation:**
- Cost: Haiku for PM planning vs Sonnet for chat (but you could use tool-level model routing instead)
- The structured output constraint (`pmAgentResultSchema`) ensures PM always returns a well-formed suggestion list

I'd lean toward flattening `suggest_work_items` and `create_work_item` directly into the chat agent. The structured suggestion format can be enforced at the tool level rather than the agent level. What do you think?

> DEVELOPER

but the thing is: we're gonna have more subagents that need to use these pm tools

> AGENT

Ah, that changes the picture completely. If multiple agents need `suggest_work_items` and `create_work_item`, then flattening them into the chat agent is wrong — you'd end up duplicating them into every future subagent.

The right move is to make the PM tools available as standalone tools (like `search_entities` and `create_observation` already are), and let any agent compose them into its toolset. That's the MCP-server-like model you were describing — tools as a shared capability layer, agents as consumers.

So instead of:

```
Chat Agent → invoke_pm_agent tool → PM Agent (separate LLM call with its own tools)
```

It becomes:

```
Chat Agent        → { search_entities, suggest_work_items, create_work_item, ... }
Future Agent X    → { suggest_work_items, create_work_item, ... }
Future Agent Y    → { create_work_item, create_observation, ... }
```

The `invoke_pm_agent` tool, `runPmAgent()`, `buildPmSystemPrompt()`, the intent routing, and the separate Haiku call all go away. The structured output schema (`pmAgentResultSchema`) was only needed because a separate LLM was producing the suggestions — with flattened tools, each tool enforces its own output shape.

The only thing lost is the cost optimization of routing PM work to Haiku. But that's a model routing concern, not an agent architecture concern — and it doesn't justify a whole separate agent boundary.

Want me to go ahead with this? The plan would be:

1. Move `suggest_work_items` and `create_work_item` from `agents/pm/` into `chat/tools/` (shared tool layer)
2. Add them to `createChatAgentTools()`
3. Remove `invoke_pm_agent` tool, `runPmAgent()`, `buildPmSystemPrompt()`, and the `agents/pm/` directory
4. Remove `pmModel` from deps (no separate model needed)
5. Update AGENTS.md to reflect the flat tool architecture

1. **Dynamic context loading** — fetches workspace projects and open observations from the graph (lines 34-45). This is real work that needs to happen somewhere.

2. **PM domain knowledge** — entity classification rules, dedup rules, when to suggest vs create directly, observation severity guidelines, etc. (lines 58-115)

If you flatten the PM tools into the chat agent, this guidance has two places it could live:

**Option A: Tool-level `.describe()`** — Put the classification, dedup, and creation rules on the `suggest_work_items` and `create_work_item` tool/parameter descriptions. The LLM already sees these via the tools API. This follows your existing convention in AGENTS.md ("parameter-level guidance belongs on `.describe()` in the Zod `inputSchema`"). The dynamic context (projects, observations) is already loaded by `buildChatContext()` for the chat agent.

**Option B: Merge into chat agent system prompt** — Add a PM responsibilities section to `buildSystemPrompt()`. But this violates your own rule: "Do NOT duplicate tool descriptions or parameter guidance in the system prompt."

Option A seems right. The entity classification, priority rules, and dedup logic are all parameter-level guidance for `suggest_work_items` and `create_work_item`. The dynamic context (projects list, observations) is already in the chat agent's context via `buildChatContext()`.

You're right — I was conflating two separate concerns.

**Tools being shared** and **having a PM subagent** aren't mutually exclusive. The AI SDK subagent pattern actually solves both:

1. **Tools stay shared** — `suggest_work_items`, `create_work_item`, etc. live in `chat/tools/` as composable building blocks. Any agent can include them.

2. **PM subagent keeps its system prompt** — the entity classification rules, dedup logic, and dynamic context (projects, observations) are genuinely valuable as a focused system prompt for PM-scoped work. That's what subagents are for — isolated context with specialized instructions.

3. **The AI SDK `createSubagent()` replaces the hand-rolled version** — instead of `invoke_pm_agent` calling `generateText()` manually, you get proper streaming, `toModelOutput` for context control, and the standard tool-based invocation pattern.

So the refactor would be:

```
Before (hand-rolled):
  invoke_pm_agent tool → runPmAgent() → generateText() with hardcoded tools

After (AI SDK subagent + shared tools):
  PM subagent (createSubagent) → composes shared tools from chat/tools/
  Future subagent X             → composes same shared tools
  Chat agent                    → can also use the same tools directly
```

The PM system prompt stays. The PM tools move to the shared layer. The invocation becomes a proper AI SDK subagent instead of a hand-rolled `generateText` call. Want me to plan this out?
