Perfect! I now have all the information I need. Let me provide you with a comprehensive analysis of the AI SDK subagent pattern.

## Summary: Vercel AI SDK Subagent Pattern

Based on my exploration of your codebase and the AI SDK v6.0.101, here's what I found:

### 1. AI SDK Version & Package

Your project uses **`ai` v6.0.101** from the Vercel AI SDK. This version does NOT have a dedicated `createSubagent()` function or explicit subagent API. Instead, subagents are implemented using the existing **`Agent` interface** and **`ToolLoopAgent`** class.

### 2. Subagent Pattern in the Vercel AI SDK

The AI SDK exports:
- **`Agent` interface** — defines the subagent contract (v1 spec)
- **`ToolLoopAgent` class** — concrete implementation of the Agent interface
- **`generateText()` and `streamText()` functions** — entry points for orchestrating agents

**Agent Interface (from the SDK):**
```typescript
interface Agent<CALL_OPTIONS = never, TOOLS extends ToolSet = {}, OUTPUT extends Output = never> {
  readonly version: 'agent-v1';
  readonly id: string | undefined;
  readonly tools: TOOLS;
  
  generate(options: AgentCallParameters<CALL_OPTIONS, TOOLS>): PromiseLike<GenerateTextResult<TOOLS, OUTPUT>>;
  stream(options: AgentStreamParameters<CALL_OPTIONS, TOOLS>): PromiseLike<StreamTextResult<TOOLS, OUTPUT>>;
}
```

**ToolLoopAgent Class:**
- Implements the `Agent` interface
- Runs tools in a loop until stop condition (default: 20 steps, customizable with `stopWhen`)
- Continues loop until: finish reason != tool-calls, tool has no execute function, tool call needs approval, or stop condition met

### 3. Current Implementation in Your Codebase

Your codebase demonstrates a **thin orchestrator pattern** (as documented in AGENTS.md):

**Chat Agent Architecture:**
```
User Message
  ├─→ Extraction Pipeline (Haiku, always runs)
  └─→ Chat Agent (Sonnet, thin orchestrator via streamText)
        ├─→ Direct tools (search, entity detail, decisions, observations)
        └─→ Subagent dispatch
              └─→ PM Agent (Haiku)
```

**Key Files:**

| File | Role |
|------|------|
| `app/src/server/chat/handler.ts` | Main chat agent orchestrator using `streamText()` with `stepCountIs(5)` |
| `app/src/server/agents/pm/agent.ts` | PM subagent using `generateText()` with `stepCountIs(6)` |
| `app/src/server/chat/tools/invoke-pm-agent.ts` | Chat agent tool that dispatches to PM subagent |
| `app/src/server/chat/tools/types.ts` | Defines `ChatToolExecutionContext` (actor-typed context) |

### 4. Current Imports from `ai`

Your codebase currently uses:

```typescript
import { generateText, Output, stepCountIs } from "ai";        // PM agent
import { stepCountIs, streamText, type ModelMessage } from "ai";  // Chat agent
import { tool } from "ai";                                     // Tool definitions
import { generateObject } from "ai";                           // For extraction
import type { ToolExecutionOptions } from "ai";               // Tool context
```

### 5. How Subagent Dispatch Works in Your Code

**PM Agent Invocation (Non-streaming, structured output):**
```typescript
// app/src/server/agents/pm/agent.ts
export async function runPmAgent(input: PmAgentInput): Promise<PmAgentOutput> {
  const result = await generateText({
    model: input.deps.pmModel,
    system: await buildPmSystemPrompt(...),
    prompt: "...",
    tools: createPmTools(input.deps),  // PM-specific tools
    output: Output.object({ schema: pmAgentResultSchema }),  // Structured output
    experimental_context: input.context,  // Pass actor context
    stopWhen: stepCountIs(6),  // Stop after 6 steps
  });

  // Extract tool results across all steps
  for (const step of result.steps) {
    for (const toolResult of step.toolResults) {
      // Collect extracted entities/relationships
    }
  }
  
  return { ...result.output, extracted_entities, extracted_relationships };
}
```

**Chat Agent Dispatch to PM Subagent:**
```typescript
// app/src/server/chat/tools/invoke-pm-agent.ts
export function createInvokePmAgentTool(deps: ChatAgentToolDeps) {
  return tool({
    description: "Invoke the PM subagent...",
    inputSchema: z.object({
      intent: z.enum(["plan_work", "check_status", "organize", "track_dependencies"]),
      context: z.string(),
      project: z.string().optional(),
    }),
    execute: async (input, options) => {
      const context = requireToolContext(options);  // Extract actor context
      
      return runPmAgent({
        deps,
        context,
        intent: input.intent,
        conversationContext: input.context,
      });
    },
  });
}
```

**Chat Agent (Streaming, orchestrator):**
```typescript
// app/src/server/chat/handler.ts
const result = streamText({
  model: input.model,  // Sonnet (chat agent)
  system: buildSystemPrompt(context, ...),
  messages: modelMessages,
  tools: createChatAgentTools({...}),  // Includes invoke_pm_agent tool
  experimental_context: {
    actor: "chat_agent",
    workspaceRecord: input.workspaceRecord,
    conversationRecord: input.conversationRecord,
    currentMessageRecord: input.currentMessageRecord,
    latestUserText: input.latestUserText,
  },
  stopWhen: stepCountIs(5),  // Chat agent stops after 5 steps
});
```

### 6. Context Passing Pattern

Your codebase uses `experimental_context` to pass actor-typed context to tools:

```typescript
// Define context type
export type ChatToolExecutionContext = {
  actor: "chat_agent" | "mcp" | "pm_agent";
  workspaceRecord: RecordId<"workspace", string>;
  conversationRecord: RecordId<"conversation", string>;
  currentMessageRecord: RecordId<"message", string>;
  latestUserText: string;
  workspaceOwnerRecord?: RecordId<"person", string>;
};

// Helper to extract it in tool execution
export function requireToolContext(options: ToolExecutionOptions): ChatToolExecutionContext {
  const context = options.experimental_context as ChatToolExecutionContext | undefined;
  if (!context) {
    throw new Error("chat tool execution context is missing");
  }
  return context;
}
```

### 7. Key Differences from Traditional Agent Frameworks

Unlike frameworks like LangGraph, the Vercel AI SDK subagent pattern:
- ✅ **No explicit subagent creation function** — agents are implicit via `generateText()` / `streamText()` calls
- ✅ **Graph-based communication** — subagents don't pass data directly; they use the knowledge graph (SurrealDB) as the bus
- ✅ **Tool-driven orchestration** — chat agent calls PM subagent via tool, not direct function call
- ✅ **Structured output** — PM agent returns `Output.object({ schema })` for strict JSON parsing
- ✅ **Step counting** — `stepCountIs(n)` replaces traditional "max_iterations" (steps include model calls + tool executions)
- ✅ **Experimental context** — `experimental_context` passes actor info through tool execution options

### 8. Tool Exports Available in AI SDK v6.0.101

The SDK also exports:
- `tool()` — create Zod-based tools (you're using this)
- `dynamicTool()` — JSON schema tools
- `Output.object()`, `Output.text()`, etc. — structured output variants
- `ToolLoopAgent` — if you want to implement agents as classes instead of functions
- `Agent` interface — for custom agent implementations

**Notably absent:** `createSubagent()`, `createAgent()`, or any explicit subagent factory. The pattern is implicit through `generateText` / `streamText` calls with tools.

---

## Key Files to Reference

1. **Agent entry point:** `/Users/marcus/conductor/workspaces/brain/dubai-v1/app/src/server/agents/pm/agent.ts` (PM subagent implementation)
2. **Orchestrator:** `/Users/marcus/conductor/workspaces/brain/dubai-v1/app/src/server/chat/handler.ts` (chat agent with dispatch)
3. **Dispatch tool:** `/Users/marcus/conductor/workspaces/brain/dubai-v1/app/src/server/chat/tools/invoke-pm-agent.ts` (how chat invokes PM)

## PM Agent Infrastructure Exploration - Complete Report

I have conducted a thorough exploration of the PM agent infrastructure and all its consumers in `/Users/marcus/conductor/workspaces/brain/dubai-v1/`. Here are my comprehensive findings:

### 1. **Full `/agents/pm/` Directory Structure**

The PM agent directory contains **5 core files**:

#### **`agent.ts`** (115 lines)
- **Entry point**: `runPmAgent(input: PmAgentInput)` function
- **Responsibilities**:
  - Invokes `generateText()` from Vercel AI SDK with structured output
  - Uses `pmAgentResultSchema` to enforce strict JSON output shape
  - Collects extracted entities and relationships from tool results across all steps
  - Implements step limit of 6 max steps via `stepCountIs(6)`
  - Returns `PmAgentOutput` = parsed result + extracted entities/relationships
- **Input Type**: `PmAgentInput`
  ```typescript
  type PmAgentInput = {
    deps: ChatToolDeps & { pmModel: any };        // Note: just ChatToolDeps + pmModel
    context: ChatToolExecutionContext;
    intent: "plan_work" | "check_status" | "organize" | "track_dependencies";
    conversationContext: string;
    project?: string;
  };
  ```
- **Output Type**: `PmAgentOutput`
  ```typescript
  type PmAgentOutput = PmAgentResult & {
    extracted_entities: ExtractedEntity[];
    extracted_relationships: ExtractedRelationship[];
  };
  ```
- **Result Schema**: 
  ```typescript
  type PmAgentResult = {
    summary: string;                        // Top-level summary
    suggestions: WorkItemSuggestion[];       // New work items to create
    updated: Array<{existing_id, title, changes}>; // Items to merge into
    discarded: Array<{title, reason}>;      // Duplicates to skip
    observations_created: string[];          // Observation IDs created
  };
  ```
- **Intent-based routing**: Each intent (plan_work, check_status, organize, track_dependencies) has specific instructions in `INTENT_INSTRUCTIONS` map

#### **`prompt.ts`** (117 lines)
- **Purpose**: Builds the PM agent system prompt dynamically
- **Loads dynamic context**:
  - Workspace projects (up to 20) via `listWorkspaceProjectSummaries()`
  - Active observations (up to 20) via `listWorkspaceOpenObservations()`
- **Covers these topics**:
  - Graph-as-communication-bus architecture (agents read/write graph independently)
  - Entity kind classification (Project, Feature, Task)
  - When to create directly vs suggest (explicit user requests vs brainstorming)
  - Category classification (engineering, research, marketing, operations, design, sales)
  - Priority classification (critical, high, medium, low)
  - What NOT to create (vague refs, questions-as-features, hypotheticals)
  - Task suggestion rules (dedup at 0.97 exact, merge at ≥0.8, keep <0.8)
  - Feature management (decompose into tasks, track blockers)
  - Project status rules (when intent=check_status)
  - Observation rules (severity: conflict/warning/info, lifecycle: open→acknowledged→resolved)

#### **`tools.ts`** (16 lines)
- **Purpose**: Factory function to create PM-specific tools
- **Signature**: `createPmTools(deps: ChatToolDeps) → ToolSet`
- **Creates these tools**:
  1. `search_entities` (from chat tools)
  2. `get_project_status` (from chat tools)
  3. `create_observation` (from chat tools)
  4. **`suggest_work_items`** (PM-specific)
  5. **`create_work_item`** (PM-specific)
- **Key insight**: PM tools reuse shared chat tools for search/status/observations, adding two PM-only tools

#### **`suggest-work-items.ts`** (129 lines) — PM-Specific Tool
- **Purpose**: Process batches of proposed work items with dedup logic
- **Signature**: 
  ```typescript
  function createSuggestWorkItemsTool(deps: ChatToolDeps) → Tool
  ```
- **Input**: Array of items (1-25) with kind, title, rationale, optional category/project/priority
- **Output buckets**:
  - **Suggestions**: New items (no close match found)
  - **Updated**: Items with ≥0.8 embedding similarity to open/in_progress items (marked for merge)
  - **Discarded**: Items with >0.97 similarity (exact duplicate of open item)
- **Dedup thresholds**:
  - `>0.97` = exact duplicate → discard with "Exact duplicate of existing item" message
  - `≥0.8` = significant overlap → mark as "merge into existing" update
  - `<0.8` = new scope → suggest as new item
  - **Closed items** (status in {done, completed, closed, resolved, superseded}) → treat as new scope even if similar
- **Implementation**:
  - Creates embeddings for each item title via `createEmbeddingVector()`
  - Searches workspace entities by embedding via `searchEntitiesByEmbedding()` with kind filter
  - Checks project scope if provided via `resolveWorkspaceProjectRecord()`
  - Does NOT persist anything—just returns triage buckets for PM agent to handle

#### **`create-work-item.ts`** (197 lines) — PM-Specific Tool
- **Purpose**: Direct entity creation in graph (used when user explicitly requests)
- **Signature**:
  ```typescript
  function createCreateWorkItemTool(deps: ChatToolDeps & { extractionModel: any }) → Tool
  ```
- **Note**: Requires `extractionModel` (for firing description update triggers), not `pmModel`
- **Input**: kind, title, rationale, optional category/priority/project
- **Persists immediately**:
  - **Task**: `RecordId("task", uuid)` with title, status="open", category/priority/embedding
  - **Feature**: `RecordId("feature", uuid)` with name, status="active", category/embedding
  - **Project**: Uses `createProjectRecord()` helper
- **Side effects**:
  - Creates embedding and stores on entity record
  - Optionally links to project via `belongs_to` (task) or `has_feature` (feature) relation
  - Seeds description entry via `seedDescriptionEntry()` (async, best-effort)
  - Creates extraction provenance edge with confidence 0.95
  - For features: fires description update triggers via `fireDescriptionUpdates()`
- **Key difference from suggest**: Creates immediately, doesn't deduplicate

### 2. **How `pmModel` Flows Through the System**

#### **Config & Environment** (`runtime/config.ts`)
```typescript
export type ServerConfig = {
  pmModelId: string;  // Env: PM_MODEL (optional; defaults to EXTRACTION_MODEL)
  // ...
};

function loadServerConfig(): ServerConfig {
  const pmModelId = Bun.env.PM_MODEL && Bun.env.PM_MODEL.trim().length > 0
    ? Bun.env.PM_MODEL.trim()
    : extractionModelId;  // Falls back to extraction model if not set
  // ...
}
```
- **Env variable**: `PM_MODEL` (optional; if blank/missing, defaults to `EXTRACTION_MODEL`)
- **Config object**: Stores as `pmModelId: string`

#### **Runtime Dependencies** (`runtime/dependencies.ts`)
```typescript
export async function createRuntimeDependencies(
  config: ServerConfig
): Promise<{
  pmModel: any;  // Created here
  // ...
}> {
  const openrouter = createOpenRouter({ apiKey: config.openRouterApiKey });
  const pmModel = openrouter(config.pmModelId, {
    plugins: [{ id: "response-healing" }],
    ...(config.openRouterReasoning ? { extraBody: { reasoning: config.openRouterReasoning } } : {}),
  });
  return { pmModel, /* ... */ };
}
```
- **Creates** OpenRouter model instance using `config.pmModelId`
- **Adds response-healing plugin** (same as extraction/assistant models)
- **Passes through OpenRouter reasoning** options if configured

#### **Server Dependencies Type** (`runtime/types.ts`)
```typescript
export type ServerDependencies = {
  config: ServerConfig;
  pmModel: any;
  // ... other models
};
```

#### **Server Startup** (`runtime/start-server.ts`)
```typescript
export async function startServer(): Promise<void> {
  const config = loadServerConfig();
  const runtime = await createRuntimeDependencies(config);
  
  const deps: ServerDependencies = {
    config,
    pmModel: runtime.pmModel,  // Passed into ServerDependencies
    // ...
  };
  
  // Passed to all route handlers
  const workspaceHandlers = createWorkspaceRouteHandlers(deps);
  const chatHandlers = createChatIngressHandlers(deps);
  // ...
  
  logInfo("server.started", "...", {
    pmModelId: config.pmModelId,  // Logged at startup
    // ...
  });
}
```

#### **Chat Ingress → Chat Processor** (`chat/chat-processor.ts`)
```typescript
export async function processChatMessage(input: {
  deps: ServerDependencies;  // Full deps passed in
  // ...
}): Promise<void> {
  // ...
  const graphAwareResponse = await runChatAgent({
    model: input.deps.assistantModel,
    pmModel: input.deps.pmModel,  // Explicitly passed to chat agent
    embeddingModel: input.deps.embeddingModel,
    // ...
  });
}
```

#### **Chat Agent → Tool Creation** (`chat/handler.ts`)
```typescript
export async function runChatAgent(input: {
  pmModel: any;  // Received as parameter
  // ...
}): Promise<ChatAgentResult> {
  const result = streamText({
    model: input.model,  // Assistant model
    tools: createChatAgentTools({
      pmModel: input.pmModel,  // Passed to tools creator
      // ...
    }),
    // ...
  });
}
```

#### **Tool Factory** (`chat/tools/index.ts`)
```typescript
export function createChatAgentTools(deps: ChatAgentToolDeps) {
  return {
    // ...
    invoke_pm_agent: createInvokePmAgentTool(deps),  // deps includes pmModel
    // ...
  };
}
```

#### **invoke_pm_agent Tool** (`chat/tools/invoke-pm-agent.ts`)
```typescript
export function createInvokePmAgentTool(deps: ChatAgentToolDeps) {
  return tool({
    execute: async (input, options) => {
      const context = requireToolContext(options);
      return runPmAgent({
        deps,  // Full deps passed to PM agent
        context,
        intent: input.intent,
        conversationContext: input.context,
      });
    },
  });
}
```

#### **PM Agent Uses pmModel** (`agents/pm/agent.ts`)
```typescript
export async function runPmAgent(input: PmAgentInput): Promise<PmAgentOutput> {
  const system = await buildPmSystemPrompt({
    surreal: input.deps.surreal,
    workspaceRecord: input.context.workspaceRecord,
  });

  const result = await generateText({
    model: input.deps.pmModel,  // <-- Used here
    system,
    prompt: [/* ... */],
    tools: createPmTools(input.deps),
    output: Output.object({ schema: pmAgentResultSchema }),
    // ...
  });
}
```

**Summary of flow**:
```
ENV: PM_MODEL
  ↓
config.pmModelId
  ↓
createRuntimeDependencies() → runtime.pmModel
  ↓
ServerDependencies.pmModel
  ↓
chat/chat-processor.ts: runChatAgent({pmModel})
  ↓
chat/handler.ts: createChatAgentTools({pmModel})
  ↓
chat/tools/invoke-pm-agent.ts: createInvokePmAgentTool(deps)
  ↓
agents/pm/agent.ts: runPmAgent({deps.pmModel})
  ↓
generateText({ model: pmModel })
```

### 3. **`suggest-work-items.ts` and `create-work-item.ts` — PM-Specific Tools**

| Aspect | `suggest-work-items.ts` | `create-work-item.ts` |
|--------|------------------------|----------------------|
| **Purpose** | Batch triage (dedup + classify) | Direct entity creation |
| **When used** | PM agent brainstorming/uncertain | User explicit: "add a task..." |
| **Persists to DB** | NO — returns triage buckets | YES — creates immediately |
| **Dedup logic** | >0.97=discard, ≥0.8=merge, <0.8=suggest | None |
| **Embedding search** | Yes, via `searchEntitiesByEmbedding()` | Yes, to create embedding for entity |
| **Module requires** | Only `ChatToolDeps` | `ChatToolDeps & { extractionModel }` |
| **Return shape** | `{ suggestions, updated, discarded }` | `{ entity_id, kind, title }` |
| **Entities created** | None | 1: task, feature, or project |
| **Confidence score** | N/A | 0.95 (fixed) in provenance edge |
| **Side effects** | None | seedDescriptionEntry, createExtractionProvenanceEdge, fireDescriptionUpdates (for features) |

**Key insight**: `suggest-work-items` is a **triage/dedup tool** (returns buckets), while `create-work-item` is a **persistence tool** (creates immediately).

### 4. **`WorkItemSuggestionList` Component Rendering**

#### **System Prompt Instructions** (`chat/context.ts`, lines 256–260)
```typescript
"### Rendering PM Suggestions",
"When invoke_pm_agent returns suggestions, render them as a WorkItemSuggestionList:",
'```component WorkItemSuggestionList { "title": "Suggested Work Items", "items": [{"kind": "task", "title": "...", "rationale": "..."}] }```',
"Include all fields from the PM result: kind, title, rationale, project, priority, category.",
"If a suggestion has a possible_duplicate, include possibleDuplicateId, possibleDuplicateName, possibleDuplicateSimilarity.",
```

The prompt instructs the chat agent to:
1. Call `invoke_pm_agent` tool (which runs `runPmAgent()`)
2. Extract `.suggestions` from the result
3. Render using **component syntax** `WorkItemSuggestionList`
4. Pass title + items (with kind, title, rationale, and optional fields)

#### **Component Definition** (`shared/chat-component-definitions.ts`)
```typescript
export const workItemSuggestionListPropsSchema = z.object({
  title: z.string().min(1),
  items: z.array(workItemSuggestionPropsSchema).min(1),
});

export const chatComponentDefinitions = {
  WorkItemSuggestionList: {
    description: "Renders a list of PM work-item suggestions with Accept All/Dismiss All controls.",
    props: workItemSuggestionListPropsSchema,
  },
};
```

#### **Client-Side Rendering** (`client/chat-component-catalog.tsx`, lines 184–235)
```typescript
function WorkItemSuggestionList(props: WorkItemSuggestionListProps) {
  const workspaceId = useWorkspaceState((s) => s.workspaceId);
  const [allStatus, setAllStatus] = useState<"idle" | "accepting">("idle");
  const [dismissedAll, setDismissedAll] = useState(false);

  async function handleAcceptAll() {
    // Batch accept all items via /api/workspaces/:workspaceId/work-items/accept
    await Promise.all(props.items.map((item) => acceptWorkItem(workspaceId, item)));
  }

  function handleDismissAll() {
    setDismissedAll(true);  // Hide entire list
  }

  return (
    <section className="work-item-suggestion-list">
      <div className="work-item-suggestion-list-header">
        <p className="extraction-summary-title">{props.title}</p>
        <div className="work-item-actions">
          <button onClick={handleAcceptAll} disabled={allStatus === "accepting"}>
            {allStatus === "accepting" ? "Accepting..." : "Accept All"}
          </button>
          <button onClick={handleDismissAll}>Dismiss All</button>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div className="extraction-summary-grid">
        {props.items.map((item) => (
          <WorkItemSuggestion key={`${item.kind}:${item.title}`} {...item} />
        ))}
      </div>
    </section>
  );
}
```

**Individual item rendering**:
```typescript
function WorkItemSuggestion(props: WorkItemSuggestionProps) {
  const [status, setStatus] = useState<WorkItemStatus>("pending");

  async function handleAccept() {
    setStatus("accepting");
    await acceptWorkItem(workspaceId, props);  // POST to accept endpoint
    setStatus("accepted");
  }

  return (
    <article className={`work-item-suggestion ${status === "accepted" ? "work-item-suggestion--accepted" : ""}`}>
      <div className="work-item-suggestion-header">
        <span className="entity-kind">{props.kind === "task" ? "Task" : "Feature"}</span>
        {props.category && <CategoryBadge category={props.category} />}
        {props.priority && <span className="work-item-priority">{props.priority}</span>}
        <div className="work-item-actions">
          <button className="work-item-btn work-item-btn--accept" onClick={handleAccept}>Accept</button>
          <button className="work-item-btn work-item-btn--dismiss" onClick={handleDismiss}>Dismiss</button>
        </div>
      </div>
      <p className="entity-name">{props.title}</p>
      <p className="work-item-rationale">{props.rationale}</p>
      {isDuplicate && <p className="work-item-duplicate-hint">Similar to existing: {props.possibleDuplicateName} ({similarity}% match)</p>}
      {props.project && <p className="work-item-project">Project: {props.project}</p>}
    </article>
  );
}
```

**Flow**:
1. PM agent returns `{ suggestions: [...], updated: [...], discarded: [...] }`
2. Chat agent renders suggestions as `WorkItemSuggestionList` component
3. User clicks "Accept All" or individual "Accept" buttons
4. Client calls `POST /api/workspaces/:workspaceId/work-items/accept`
5. Endpoint creates task/feature/project in DB
6. Item UI shows "Accepted" badge

### 5. **Tests for PM Agent Functionality**

#### **Smoke Test: PM Agent Task Creation** (`tests/smoke/pipeline.test.ts`, lines 133–187)
```typescript
it("PM agent creates task from explicit request", async () => {
  const { baseUrl, surreal } = getRuntime();
  const workspace = await createOnboardedWorkspace(baseUrl, surreal);

  // Seed a project first
  const projectRecord = new RecordId("project", randomUUID());
  const workspaceRecord = new RecordId("workspace", workspace.workspaceId);
  await surreal.create(projectRecord).content({
    name: "Brain Platform",
    status: "active",
    workspace: workspaceRecord,
    created_at: new Date(),
    updated_at: new Date(),
  });
  await surreal.relate(workspaceRecord, new RecordId("has_project", randomUUID()), projectRecord, {
    added_at: new Date(),
  }).output("after");

  // Send explicit creation request
  const message = await fetchJson<ChatMessageResponse>(`${baseUrl}/api/chat/messages`, {
    method: "POST",
    body: JSON.stringify({
      clientMessageId: randomUUID(),
      workspaceId: workspace.workspaceId,
      conversationId: workspace.conversationId,
      text: "Add a task for implementing user authentication for Brain Platform.",
    }),
  });

  const events = await collectSseEvents<StreamEvent>(`${baseUrl}${message.streamUrl}`, 120_000);
  const assistantEvent = events.find((event) => event.type === "assistant_message");

  // Verify task created OR suggestion rendered
  const [taskRows] = await surreal.query<[Array<{id, title}>]>(
    "SELECT id, title FROM task WHERE workspace = $workspace;",
    { workspace: workspaceRecord },
  ).collect();

  const hasTaskInDb = taskRows.length > 0;
  const hasSuggestionInResponse = assistantEvent.text.includes("WorkItemSuggestionList");
  expect(hasTaskInDb || hasSuggestionInResponse).toBe(true);
});
```

**What it tests**:
- Chat agent interprets explicit creation request ("Add a task...")
- Either creates task directly in DB via PM agent's `create_work_item` tool
- OR returns PM suggestions as `WorkItemSuggestionList` for user acceptance
- Verifies task created in workspace scope

**No unit tests** found specifically for PM agent (only smoke test exists).

### 6. **`ChatAgentToolDeps` vs `ChatToolDeps` — Type Difference**

#### **`ChatToolDeps`** (`chat/tools/types.ts`, lines 12–19)
```typescript
export type ChatToolDeps = {
  surreal: Surreal;
  embeddingModel: any;
  embeddingDimension: number;
  extractionModelId: string;
  extractionModel: any;
  extractionStoreThreshold: number;
};
```
**Used by**: Shared chat tools (search, get_project_status, create_observation) and PM tools

#### **`ChatAgentToolDeps`** (`chat/tools/types.ts`, lines 21–23)
```typescript
export type ChatAgentToolDeps = ChatToolDeps & {
  pmModel: any;
};
```
**Used by**: Chat agent tools (includes PM subagent invocation)

#### **Why This Distinction?**

| Context | Deps Type | Why |
|---------|-----------|-----|
| **Chat agent** (main orchestrator) | `ChatAgentToolDeps` | Needs `pmModel` to invoke subagent |
| **PM agent** (subagent) | `ChatToolDeps` | Gets `pmModel` from caller via `input.deps.pmModel` |
| **Shared tools** (search, status, etc.) | `ChatToolDeps` | Don't need to invoke subagents |
| **Work item accept endpoint** | Uses `ServerDependencies` | Different context (HTTP handler) |

**Key insight**: 
- `ChatToolDeps` is the **minimal set** all tools need (surreal, embeddings, extraction model)
- `ChatAgentToolDeps` adds `pmModel` for the **chat agent specifically** (because only chat agent invokes PM)
- **PM agent doesn't need `ChatAgentToolDeps`**—it receives `ChatToolDeps & { pmModel }` from caller
- This prevents circular dependency: PM agent (subagent) doesn't import chat agent types

**Why PM tool signature is `ChatToolDeps` not `ChatAgentToolDeps`**:
```typescript
// In agents/pm/agent.ts, line 52:
export type PmAgentInput = {
  deps: ChatToolDeps & { pmModel: any };  // <-- inline the pmModel, don't use ChatAgentToolDeps
  context: ChatToolExecutionContext;
  // ...
};

// In agents/pm/tools.ts, line 8:
export function createPmTools(deps: ChatToolDeps) {  // <-- just ChatToolDeps
  // PM tools use search, status, create_observation (all in ChatToolDeps)
  // PM also has create_work_item which needs extractionModel (in ChatToolDeps)
}

// But PM agent signature accepts pmModel via inline union:
export async function runPmAgent(input: PmAgentInput): Promise<PmAgentOutput> {
  const result = await generateText({
    model: input.deps.pmModel,  // <-- pmModel is in input.deps, not direct import
    // ...
  });
}
```

**Architecture benefit**: Keeps PM agent **decoupled from chat agent types**. PM agent only knows about `ChatToolDeps` + the inline pmModel, not `ChatAgentToolDeps`.

┌─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
│                     Server Bootstrap                             │
├─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┤

└─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘

┌─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
│                    Chat Message Flow                              │
├─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┤
│ processChatMessage(deps: ServerDependencies)                      │
│   ↓                                                               │
│ runChatAgent(pmModel: deps.pmModel)                               │
│   ↓                                                               │
│ createChatAgentTools(deps: ChatAgentToolDeps)                     │
│   ├─→ search_entities (ChatToolDeps)                              │
│   ├─→ get_project_status (ChatToolDeps)                           │
│   ├─→ create_observation (ChatToolDeps)                           │
│   ├─→ create_provisional_decision (ChatToolDeps)                  │
│   ├─→ create_question (ChatToolDeps)                              │
│   └─→ invoke_pm_agent (ChatAgentToolDeps)                         │
│         ↓                                                          │
│         runPmAgent(deps: ChatToolDeps & {pmModel})                │
│           ├─→ createPmTools(ChatToolDeps)                         │
│           │   ├─→ search_entities                                 │
│           │   ├─→ get_project_status                              │
│           │   ├─→ create_observation                              │
│           │   ├─→ suggest_work_items   ← Triage/dedup, no persist │
│           │   └─→ create_work_item     ← Direct creation          │
│           └─→ generateText(model: pmModel)                        │
│               ↓                                                    │
│               { summary, suggestions, updated, discarded, ... }   │
│                 ↓                                                  │

│                 ↓                                                  │
│               User clicks "Accept" / "Accept All"                 │
│                 ↓                                                  │
│               POST /api/workspaces/:workspaceId/work-items/accept │
│                 ↓                                                  │
│               Create task/feature/project in DB                   │
└─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘

| File Path | Purpose | Key exports |

| `app/src/server/agents/pm/prompt.ts` | Dynamic system prompt | `buildPmSystemPrompt()` |

## Summary

I've completed a thorough analysis of the Vercel AI SDK's Agent/subagent API surface (v6.0.101). Here are the key findings:

### 1. **ToolLoopAgent Class**

**Location:** `/Users/marcus/conductor/workspaces/brain/dubai-v1/node_modules/ai/src/agent/tool-loop-agent.ts`

**Constructor Signature:**

**Full Type Definition:**
```typescript
export class ToolLoopAgent<
  CALL_OPTIONS = never,
  TOOLS extends ToolSet = {},
  OUTPUT extends Output = never,
> implements Agent<CALL_OPTIONS, TOOLS, OUTPUT> {
  readonly version = 'agent-v1';
  constructor(settings: ToolLoopAgentSettings<CALL_OPTIONS, TOOLS, OUTPUT>);
  get id(): string | undefined;
  get tools(): TOOLS;
  async generate(options: AgentCallParameters<CALL_OPTIONS, TOOLS>): Promise<GenerateTextResult<TOOLS, OUTPUT>>;
  async stream(options: AgentStreamParameters<CALL_OPTIONS, TOOLS>): Promise<StreamTextResult<TOOLS, OUTPUT>>;
}
```

**Exported As:** Both `ToolLoopAgent` and `Experimental_Agent` (deprecated alias)

### 2. **ToolLoopAgentSettings Type** (Constructor Parameters)

**Location:** `/Users/marcus/conductor/workspaces/brain/dubai-v1/node_modules/ai/src/agent/tool-loop-agent-settings.ts`

**Core Required Parameters:**
- `model: LanguageModel` - **REQUIRED** - The language model to use
- `id?: string` - Optional agent identifier
- `instructions?: string | SystemModelMessage | Array<SystemModelMessage>` - System prompt
- `tools?: TOOLS` - Tools available to the agent
- `toolChoice?: ToolChoice<TOOLS>` - Tool selection strategy (default: 'auto')
- `stopWhen?: StopCondition<TOOLS> | Array<StopCondition<TOOLS>>` - Stopping conditions (default: `stepCountIs(20)`)
- `output?: OUTPUT` - Optional structured output specification

**Optional Callback Parameters:**
- `experimental_onStart?: ToolLoopAgentOnStartCallback<TOOLS, OUTPUT>` - Called before any LLM calls
- `experimental_onStepStart?: ToolLoopAgentOnStepStartCallback<TOOLS, OUTPUT>` - Called before each LLM step
- `experimental_onToolCallStart?: ToolLoopAgentOnToolCallStartCallback<TOOLS>` - Called before tool execution
- `experimental_onToolCallFinish?: ToolLoopAgentOnToolCallFinishCallback<TOOLS>` - Called after tool execution
- `onStepFinish?: ToolLoopAgentOnStepFinishCallback<TOOLS>` - Called when each step finishes
- `onFinish?: ToolLoopAgentOnFinishCallback<TOOLS>` - Called when all steps finish

**CallSettings Inherited Parameters:**
- `maxOutputTokens?: number` - Token limit
- `temperature?: number` - Sampling temperature
- `topP?: number` - Nucleus sampling parameter

- `frequencyPenalty?: number` - Frequency penalty (-1 to 1)

- `maxRetries?: number` - Retry count (default: 2)
- `headers?: Record<string, string | undefined>` - HTTP headers

- `prepareStep?: PrepareStepFunction<TOOLS>` - Provide different settings per step
- `experimental_repairToolCall?: ToolCallRepairFunction<TOOLS>` - Repair failed tool calls
- `experimental_telemetry?: TelemetrySettings` - Telemetry configuration
- `activeTools?: Array<keyof TOOLS>` - Limit which tools are available
- `providerOptions?: ProviderOptions` - Provider-specific options
- `experimental_context?: unknown` - Context passed to tool calls
- `experimental_download?: DownloadFunction` - Custom URL downloader

export interface Agent<
  CALL_OPTIONS = never,
  TOOLS extends ToolSet = {},
  OUTPUT extends Output = never,
> {
  readonly version: 'agent-v1';
  readonly id: string | undefined;
  readonly tools: TOOLS;

  stream(options: AgentStreamParameters<CALL_OPTIONS, TOOLS>): PromiseLike<StreamTextResult<TOOLS, OUTPUT>>;
}
```

- `messages?: Array<ModelMessage>` - Alternative to prompt

- `timeout?: TimeoutConfiguration` - Timeout in milliseconds or object with `totalMs`
- Runtime callbacks (same as constructor callbacks)

### 9. **How the PM Agent Currently Uses the SDK**

From `/Users/marcus/conductor/workspaces/brain/dubai-v1/app/src/server/agents/pm/agent.ts`:

The PM agent uses **`generateText()`** (not ToolLoopAgent) with:
```typescript
const result = await generateText({
  model: input.deps.pmModel,
  system: systemPrompt,
  prompt: userMessage,
  tools: createPmTools(input.deps),
  output: Output.object({ schema: pmAgentResultSchema }),
  experimental_context: input.context,
  stopWhen: stepCountIs(6),
});
```

This is a simpler pattern than the full Agent interface - it's a direct function call that doesn't require instantiating an Agent class.

### 10. **Key Differences**

| Feature | ToolLoopAgent | generateText() |
|---------|---------------|-----------------|
| Instantiation | `new ToolLoopAgent({...})` | Direct function call |
| Reusability | Instance can be called multiple times | Single call per invocation |
| UI Streaming | Designed for `createAgentUIStream` | Uses `streamText()` directly |
| Callbacks | Settable on constructor and per-call | Per-call only |
| Tools | Stored in agent instance | Passed per-call |

All three main functions/classes are **exported from 'ai'** and can be imported as:
```typescript
import { ToolLoopAgent, readUIMessageStream, createAgentUIStream } from 'ai';

## Implementation Plan: PM Subagent Refactoring to ToolLoopAgent + Shared Tool Layer

### Overview

This refactoring has two parts:
1. **Move PM-specific tools** (`suggest-work-items.ts`, `create-work-item.ts`) from `agents/pm/` to `chat/tools/` so any future subagent can compose them.
2. **Replace `generateText()` with `ToolLoopAgent`** in `agents/pm/agent.ts` to use the AI SDK's agent abstraction.

No changes to the chat agent, the `invoke_pm_agent` tool wrapper, or the chat processor. All changes are internal to the PM agent wiring.

---

### Design Decisions

**Q1: ToolLoopAgent per-invocation or per-request?**

Per-invocation (inside `runPmAgent()`). Reasons:
- The system prompt is async -- it queries SurrealDB for workspace projects and observations, producing different instructions per workspace.
- `experimental_context` varies per invocation (different workspace, conversation, message records).
- Using `prepareCall` with `CALL_OPTIONS` to dynamically inject both would add unnecessary type complexity.
- `ToolLoopAgent` is a lightweight config wrapper -- constructing it per-invocation has no meaningful cost.
- This mirrors the current flow: build prompt, then call the model.

**Q2: How to handle the async system prompt?**

Build it before constructing the agent, pass as `instructions`:
```typescript
const system = await buildPmSystemPrompt({ surreal, workspaceRecord });
const agent = new ToolLoopAgent({
  instructions: system,
  // ...
});
```

The `instructions` property on `ToolLoopAgent` maps to `system` on `generateText`. Since we create the agent per-invocation, we can await the prompt first without any special hooks.

**Q3: Entity collection from ToolLoopAgent result?**

Same step iteration pattern. `agent.generate()` returns `GenerateTextResult<TOOLS, OUTPUT>` which has `.steps[].toolResults[].output` -- identical to the current `generateText` result. The entity collection loop is unchanged. This keeps the PM agent consistent with the chat agent handler's pattern.

---

### Step-by-Step File Changes

#### Step 1: Move `suggest-work-items.ts` to shared tool layer

**Action:** `git mv app/src/server/agents/pm/suggest-work-items.ts app/src/server/chat/tools/suggest-work-items.ts`

**Import path adjustments** (both files are 2 directories deep from `server/`, so `../../graph/` etc. resolve identically):

| Current import | New import |
|---|---|
| `../../chat/tools/helpers` | `./helpers` |
| `../../chat/tools/types` | `./types` |
| `../../graph/embeddings` | unchanged |
| `../../graph/queries` | unchanged |
| `../../../shared/contracts` | unchanged |

No logic changes. The function signature stays `createSuggestWorkItemsTool(deps: ChatToolDeps)`.

#### Step 2: Move `create-work-item.ts` to shared tool layer

**Action:** `git mv app/src/server/agents/pm/create-work-item.ts app/src/server/chat/tools/create-work-item.ts`

**Import path adjustments:**

| Current import | New import |
|---|---|
| `../../chat/tools/helpers` | `./helpers` |
| `../../chat/tools/types` | `./types` |
| `../../graph/embeddings` | unchanged |
| `../../graph/queries` | unchanged |
| `../../descriptions/persist` | unchanged |
| `../../descriptions/triggers` | unchanged |
| `../../workspace/workspace-scope` | unchanged |
| `../../../shared/contracts` | unchanged |

**Cleanup:** The function signature is currently `createCreateWorkItemTool(deps: ChatToolDeps & { extractionModel: any })`. Since `ChatToolDeps` already contains `extractionModel: any`, the intersection is redundant. Simplify to `createCreateWorkItemTool(deps: ChatToolDeps)`.

#### Step 3: Update `agents/pm/tools.ts` imports

**File:** `/Users/marcus/conductor/workspaces/brain/dubai-v1/app/src/server/agents/pm/tools.ts`

Change:
```typescript
import { createCreateWorkItemTool } from "./create-work-item";
import { createSuggestWorkItemsTool } from "./suggest-work-items";
```
To:
```typescript
import { createCreateWorkItemTool } from "../../chat/tools/create-work-item";
import { createSuggestWorkItemsTool } from "../../chat/tools/suggest-work-items";
```

No other changes. The function `createPmTools(deps: ChatToolDeps)` and its return shape are unchanged. It remains the convenience composition for the PM agent.

#### Step 4: Rewrite `agents/pm/agent.ts` to use ToolLoopAgent

**File:** `/Users/marcus/conductor/workspaces/brain/dubai-v1/app/src/server/agents/pm/agent.ts`

**Changes to imports:**
```typescript
// Before:
import { generateText, Output, stepCountIs } from "ai";

// After:
import { ToolLoopAgent, Output, stepCountIs } from "ai";
```

**Changes to `runPmAgent()`:**

Replace the `generateText()` call with `ToolLoopAgent` construction + `agent.generate()`:

```typescript
export async function runPmAgent(input: PmAgentInput): Promise<PmAgentOutput> {
  const system = await buildPmSystemPrompt({
    surreal: input.deps.surreal,
    workspaceRecord: input.context.workspaceRecord,
  });

  const agent = new ToolLoopAgent({
    id: "pm-agent",
    model: input.deps.pmModel,
    instructions: system,
    tools: createPmTools(input.deps),
    output: Output.object({ schema: pmAgentResultSchema }),
    experimental_context: input.context,
    stopWhen: stepCountIs(6),
  });

  const result = await agent.generate({
    prompt: [
      "You are handling a PM request.",
      `Intent: ${input.intent}`,
      input.project ? `Project hint: ${input.project}` : "Project hint: not provided",
      INTENT_INSTRUCTIONS[input.intent],
      "Context:",
      input.conversationContext,
    ].join("\n"),
  });

  if (!result.output) {
    throw new Error("pm agent did not produce structured output");
  }

  // Entity collection from steps (identical pattern)
  const extracted_entities: ExtractedEntity[] = [];
  const extracted_relationships: ExtractedRelationship[] = [];

  for (const step of result.steps) {
    for (const toolResult of step.toolResults) {
      const res = toolResult.output as Record<string, unknown> | undefined;
      if (res) {
        if (Array.isArray(res.extracted_entities)) {
          extracted_entities.push(...(res.extracted_entities as ExtractedEntity[]));
        }
        if (Array.isArray(res.extracted_relationships)) {
          extracted_relationships.push(...(res.extracted_relationships as ExtractedRelationship[]));
        }
      }
    }
  }

  return {
    ...result.output,
    extracted_entities,
    extracted_relationships,
  };
}
```

Everything else in the file (schemas, types, `INTENT_INSTRUCTIONS`) is unchanged.

#### Step 5: No changes to other files

These files are NOT modified:
- `chat/tools/invoke-pm-agent.ts` -- calls `runPmAgent()` whose interface is unchanged
- `chat/tools/index.ts` -- does not gain PM-specific tools (constraint: PM tools stay composed only in `agents/pm/tools.ts`)
- `chat/handler.ts` -- unchanged
- `chat/chat-processor.ts` -- unchanged
- `agents/pm/prompt.ts` -- unchanged
- `chat/tools/types.ts` -- unchanged

---

### Dependency Graph

```
Step 1 (move suggest-work-items) ─┐
                                   ├─→ Step 3 (update pm/tools.ts imports) ─→ Step 4 (ToolLoopAgent rewrite)
Step 2 (move create-work-item) ───┘
```

Steps 1 and 2 are independent and can be done in either order. Step 3 depends on both. Step 4 is independent of Steps 1-3 but should be done last to keep the diff reviewable.

---

### Verification

1. **Type check:** `bunx tsc --noEmit` -- verify all import paths resolve and `ToolLoopAgent` constructor types match.
2. **Unit tests:** `bun test tests/unit/` -- observation-queries test references `pm_agent` as a string value only, not any imports from the moved files. Should pass unchanged.
3. **Smoke tests:** `bun test tests/smoke/pipeline.test.ts` -- end-to-end pipeline test exercises the chat agent which may invoke the PM subagent. The behavioral contract is unchanged so this should pass.

---

### Risks and Mitigations

| Risk | Mitigation |
|---|---|
| `ToolLoopAgent` handles `system`/`instructions` differently than `generateText` | Verified in type definitions: `instructions` is `string \| SystemModelMessage \| Array<SystemModelMessage>`, matching `system` on `generateText` |
| `experimental_context` not forwarded to tools | Verified: `ToolLoopAgentSettings` has `experimental_context?: unknown` which is forwarded to tool execution, same as `generateText` |
| `Output.object()` behavior differs on `ToolLoopAgent` | Same `output` property type on both `ToolLoopAgentSettings` and `generateText` parameters |
| `result.steps` structure differs | `agent.generate()` returns `GenerateTextResult<TOOLS, OUTPUT>` -- same type as `generateText` return |
| Breaking other imports of moved files | Only `agents/pm/tools.ts` imports from the moved files, updated in Step 3 |

---

### Critical Files for Implementation

- `/Users/marcus/conductor/workspaces/brain/dubai-v1/app/src/server/agents/pm/agent.ts` - Core rewrite target: replace `generateText` with `ToolLoopAgent`

     1→import { tool } from "ai";
     2→import { z } from "zod";
     3→import { ENTITY_CATEGORIES, ENTITY_PRIORITIES, type EntityCategory } from "../../../shared/contracts";
     4→import { createEmbeddingVector } from "../../graph/embeddings";
     5→import { resolveWorkspaceProjectRecord, searchEntitiesByEmbedding } from "../../graph/queries";
     6→import { requireToolContext } from "../../chat/tools/helpers";
     7→import type { ChatToolDeps } from "../../chat/tools/types";
     8→
     9→type SuggestedWorkItem = {
    10→  kind: "task" | "feature" | "project";
    11→  title: string;
    12→  rationale: string;
    13→  category?: EntityCategory;
    14→  project?: string;
    15→  priority?: string;
    16→  possible_duplicate?: {
    17→    id: string;
    18→    name: string;
    19→    similarity: number;
    20→  };
    21→};
    22→
    23→type UpdatedWorkItem = {
    24→  existing_id: string;
    25→  title: string;
    26→  changes: string;
    27→};
    28→
    29→type DiscardedWorkItem = {
    30→  title: string;
    31→  reason: string;
    32→};
    33→
    34→const CLOSED_STATUSES = new Set(["done", "completed", "closed", "resolved", "superseded"]);
    35→
    36→export function createSuggestWorkItemsTool(deps: ChatToolDeps) {
    37→  return tool({
    38→    description:
    39→      "Process a batch of proposed work items and return issue-style PM triage buckets: suggestions, updated, discarded.",
    40→    inputSchema: z.object({
    41→      items: z.array(
    42→        z.object({
    43→          kind: z.enum(["task", "feature", "project"]).describe("Work item kind"),
    44→          title: z.string().min(1).describe("Concise work item title"),
    45→          rationale: z.string().min(1).describe("Why this work item is needed"),
    46→          category: z.enum(ENTITY_CATEGORIES).optional().describe("Optional work item category"),
    47→          project: z.string().optional().describe("Optional project scope"),
    48→          priority: z.enum(ENTITY_PRIORITIES).optional().describe("critical: blocking/urgent. high: important. medium: normal. low: nice-to-have."),
    49→        }),
    50→      ).min(1).max(25),
    51→    }),
    52→    execute: async (input, options) => {
    53→      const context = requireToolContext(options);
    54→      const suggestions: SuggestedWorkItem[] = [];
    55→      const updated: UpdatedWorkItem[] = [];
    56→      const discarded: DiscardedWorkItem[] = [];
    57→
    58→      for (const item of input.items) {
    59→        const suggestion: SuggestedWorkItem = {
    60→          kind: item.kind,
    61→          title: item.title,
    62→          rationale: item.rationale,
    63→          ...(item.category ? { category: item.category } : {}),
    64→          ...(item.project ? { project: item.project } : {}),
    65→          ...(item.priority ? { priority: item.priority } : {}),
    66→        };
    67→
    68→        const embedding = await createEmbeddingVector(deps.embeddingModel, item.title, deps.embeddingDimension);
    69→        if (!embedding) {
    70→          throw new Error("failed to create embedding for suggest_work_items");
    71→        }
    72→
    73→        const projectRecord = item.project
    74→          ? await resolveWorkspaceProjectRecord({
    75→              surreal: deps.surreal,
    76→              workspaceRecord: context.workspaceRecord,
    77→              projectInput: item.project,
    78→            })
    79→          : undefined;
    80→
    81→        const candidates = await searchEntitiesByEmbedding({
    82→          surreal: deps.surreal,
    83→          workspaceRecord: context.workspaceRecord,
    84→          queryEmbedding: embedding,
    85→          kinds: [item.kind],
    86→          ...(projectRecord ? { projectRecord } : {}),
    87→          limit: 5,
    88→        });
    89→
    90→        const top = candidates[0];
    91→        if (!top) {
    92→          suggestions.push(suggestion);
    93→          continue;
    94→        }
    95→
    96→        const normalizedTopStatus = top.status ? top.status.trim().toLowerCase() : undefined;
    97→        if (normalizedTopStatus && CLOSED_STATUSES.has(normalizedTopStatus)) {
    98→          suggestions.push(suggestion);
    99→          continue;
   100→        }
   101→
   102→        if (top.score > 0.97) {
   103→          discarded.push({
   104→            title: item.title,
   105→            reason: `Exact duplicate of existing item ${top.kind}:${top.id} (${top.name})`,
   106→          });
   107→          continue;
   108→        }
   109→
   110→        if (top.score >= 0.8) {
   111→          updated.push({
   112→            existing_id: `${top.kind}:${top.id}`,
   113→            title: top.name,
   114→            changes: `Merge context from suggested item '${item.title}'`,
   115→          });
   116→          continue;
   117→        }
   118→
   119→        suggestions.push(suggestion);
   120→      }
   121→
   122→      return {
   123→        suggestions,
   124→        updated,
   125→        discarded,
   126→      };
   127→    },
   128→  });
   129→}
   130→
```

     1→import { randomUUID } from "node:crypto";
     2→import { RecordId } from "surrealdb";
     3→import { tool } from "ai";
     4→import { z } from "zod";
     5→import { ENTITY_CATEGORIES, ENTITY_PRIORITIES } from "../../../shared/contracts";
     6→import { createEmbeddingVector } from "../../graph/embeddings";
     7→import {
     8→  createExtractionProvenanceEdge,
     9→  createProjectRecord,
    10→  resolveWorkspaceProjectRecord,
    11→} from "../../graph/queries";
    12→import { seedDescriptionEntry } from "../../descriptions/persist";
    13→import { fireDescriptionUpdates } from "../../descriptions/triggers";
    14→import { ensureProjectFeatureEdge } from "../../workspace/workspace-scope";
    15→import { requireToolContext } from "../../chat/tools/helpers";
    16→import type { ChatToolDeps } from "../../chat/tools/types";
    17→
    18→export function createCreateWorkItemTool(deps: ChatToolDeps & { extractionModel: any }) {
    19→  return tool({
    20→    description:
    21→      "Create a task, feature, or project directly in the knowledge graph. Use when the user explicitly requests creation (\"add a task for X\") or during onboarding entity seeding. For uncertain/brainstormed items, prefer suggest_work_items instead.",
    22→    inputSchema: z.object({
    23→      kind: z.enum(["task", "feature", "project"]).describe(
    24→        "task: concrete executable work with action verb. feature: capability/requirement within a project. project: named top-level workstream.",
    25→      ),
    26→      title: z.string().min(1).describe("Concise entity title"),
    27→      rationale: z.string().min(1).describe("Why this entity is needed — seeds the description"),
    28→      category: z.enum(ENTITY_CATEGORIES).optional().describe("Category classification"),
    29→      priority: z.enum(ENTITY_PRIORITIES).optional().describe("critical: blocking/urgent. high: important, needs attention soon. medium: normal priority. low: nice-to-have, deferred."),
    30→      project: z.string().optional().describe("Project name to scope the entity under"),
    31→    }),
    32→    execute: async (input, options) => {
    33→      const context = requireToolContext(options);
    34→      const now = new Date();
    35→
    36→      const embedding = await createEmbeddingVector(
    37→        deps.embeddingModel,
    38→        input.title,
    39→        deps.embeddingDimension,
    40→      );
    41→
    42→      const entityId = randomUUID();
    43→
    44→      if (input.kind === "project") {
    45→        const projectRecord = await createProjectRecord({
    46→          surreal: deps.surreal,
    47→          name: input.title,
    48→          status: "active",
    49→          now,
    50→          workspaceRecord: context.workspaceRecord,
    51→          sourceMessageRecord: context.currentMessageRecord,
    52→        });
    53→
    54→        if (embedding) {
    55→          await deps.surreal.update(projectRecord).merge({ embedding });
    56→        }
    57→
    58→        void seedDescriptionEntry({
    59→          surreal: deps.surreal,
    60→          targetRecord: projectRecord,
    61→          text: input.rationale,
    62→        }).catch(() => undefined);
    63→
    64→        await createExtractionProvenanceEdge({
    65→          surreal: deps.surreal,
    66→          sourceRecord: context.currentMessageRecord,
    67→          targetRecord: projectRecord,
    68→          now,
    69→          confidence: 0.95,
    70→          model: deps.extractionModelId,
    71→          fromText: input.title,
    72→          evidence: context.latestUserText,
    73→          evidenceSourceRecord: context.currentMessageRecord,
    74→        });
    75→
    76→        return {
    77→          entity_id: `project:${projectRecord.id as string}`,
    78→          kind: "project" as const,
    79→          title: input.title,
    80→        };
    81→      }
    82→
    83→      if (input.kind === "task") {
    84→        const taskRecord = new RecordId("task", entityId);
    85→        await deps.surreal.create(taskRecord).content({
    86→          title: input.title,
    87→          status: "open",
    88→          workspace: context.workspaceRecord,
    89→          ...(input.category ? { category: input.category } : {}),
    90→          ...(input.priority ? { priority: input.priority } : {}),
    91→          ...(embedding ? { embedding } : {}),
    92→          created_at: now,
    93→          updated_at: now,
    94→        });
    95→
    96→        if (input.project) {
    97→          try {
    98→            const projectRecord = await resolveWorkspaceProjectRecord({
    99→              surreal: deps.surreal,
   100→              workspaceRecord: context.workspaceRecord,
   101→              projectInput: input.project,
   102→            });
   103→            await deps.surreal
   104→              .relate(taskRecord, new RecordId("belongs_to", randomUUID()), projectRecord, {
   105→                added_at: now,
   106→              })
   107→              .output("after");
   108→          } catch {
   109→            // project resolution is best-effort
   110→          }
   111→        }
   112→
   113→        void seedDescriptionEntry({
   114→          surreal: deps.surreal,
   115→          targetRecord: taskRecord,
   116→          text: input.rationale,
   117→        }).catch(() => undefined);
   118→
   119→        await createExtractionProvenanceEdge({
   120→          surreal: deps.surreal,
   121→          sourceRecord: context.currentMessageRecord,
   122→          targetRecord: taskRecord,
   123→          now,
   124→          confidence: 0.95,
   125→          model: deps.extractionModelId,
   126→          fromText: input.title,
   127→          evidence: context.latestUserText,
   128→          evidenceSourceRecord: context.currentMessageRecord,
   129→        });
   130→
   131→        return {
   132→          entity_id: `task:${entityId}`,
   133→          kind: "task" as const,
   134→          title: input.title,
   135→        };
   136→      }
   137→
   138→      // feature
   139→      const featureRecord = new RecordId("feature", entityId);
   140→      await deps.surreal.create(featureRecord).content({
   141→        name: input.title,
   142→        status: "active",
   143→        ...(input.category ? { category: input.category } : {}),
   144→        ...(embedding ? { embedding } : {}),
   145→        created_at: now,
   146→        updated_at: now,
   147→      });
   148→
   149→      if (input.project) {
   150→        try {
   151→          const projectRecord = await resolveWorkspaceProjectRecord({
   152→            surreal: deps.surreal,
   153→            workspaceRecord: context.workspaceRecord,
   154→            projectInput: input.project,
   155→          });
   156→          await ensureProjectFeatureEdge(deps.surreal, projectRecord, featureRecord, now);
   157→
   158→          void fireDescriptionUpdates({
   159→            surreal: deps.surreal,
   160→            extractionModel: deps.extractionModel,
   161→            trigger: {
   162→              kind: "feature_created",
   163→              entity: featureRecord,
   164→              summary: `Feature added: ${input.title}`,
   165→            },
   166→          }).catch(() => undefined);
   167→        } catch {
   168→          // project resolution is best-effort
   169→        }
   170→      }
   171→
   172→      void seedDescriptionEntry({
   173→        surreal: deps.surreal,
   174→        targetRecord: featureRecord,
   175→        text: input.rationale,
   176→      }).catch(() => undefined);
   177→
   178→      await createExtractionProvenanceEdge({
   179→        surreal: deps.surreal,
   180→        sourceRecord: context.currentMessageRecord,
   181→        targetRecord: featureRecord,
   182→        now,
   183→        confidence: 0.95,
   184→        model: deps.extractionModelId,
   185→        fromText: input.title,
   186→        evidence: context.latestUserText,
   187→        evidenceSourceRecord: context.currentMessageRecord,
   188→      });
   189→
   190→      return {
   191→        entity_id: `feature:${entityId}`,
   192→        kind: "feature" as const,
   193→        title: input.title,
   194→      };
   195→    },
   196→  });
   197→}
   198→

## Approved Plan:
# Refactor PM Agent: Shared Tool Layer + AI SDK ToolLoopAgent

## Context

The PM agent's tools (`suggest_work_items`, `create_work_item`) live in `agents/pm/` and can only be composed by the PM agent. Future subagents will need these same tools. Additionally, the PM agent uses a hand-rolled `generateText()` call instead of the AI SDK's `ToolLoopAgent` class (available in v6.0.101).

This refactoring moves PM-specific tools to the shared `chat/tools/` layer and converts the PM agent to use `ToolLoopAgent`.

## Steps

### 1. Move `suggest-work-items.ts` to shared tool layer

`git mv app/src/server/agents/pm/suggest-work-items.ts app/src/server/chat/tools/suggest-work-items.ts`

Update two imports:
- `../../chat/tools/helpers` → `./helpers`
- `../../chat/tools/types` → `./types`

All other imports unchanged (same depth from `server/`).

### 2. Move `create-work-item.ts` to shared tool layer

`git mv app/src/server/agents/pm/create-work-item.ts app/src/server/chat/tools/create-work-item.ts`

Same two import changes as step 1. Also simplify the redundant type intersection:
- `ChatToolDeps & { extractionModel: any }` → `ChatToolDeps` (extractionModel is already in ChatToolDeps)

### 3. Update `agents/pm/tools.ts` imports

Change the two local imports to point at the new shared locations:
```typescript
import { createSuggestWorkItemsTool } from "../../chat/tools/suggest-work-items";
import { createCreateWorkItemTool } from "../../chat/tools/create-work-item";
```

The `createPmTools()` function stays as-is — it's a convenience composition for the PM agent.

### 4. Rewrite `agents/pm/agent.ts` to use ToolLoopAgent

Replace `generateText` import with `ToolLoopAgent`:
```typescript
import { ToolLoopAgent, Output, stepCountIs } from "ai";
```

In `runPmAgent()`, replace the `generateText()` call:
```typescript
const agent = new ToolLoopAgent({
  id: "pm-agent",
  model: input.deps.pmModel,
  instructions: system,
  tools: createPmTools(input.deps),
  output: Output.object({ schema: pmAgentResultSchema }),
  experimental_context: input.context,
  stopWhen: stepCountIs(6),
});

const result = await agent.generate({
  prompt: [/* same prompt lines as today */].join("\n"),
});
```

Entity collection loop from `result.steps` stays identical — `agent.generate()` returns the same `GenerateTextResult` type.

    90→### Chat Agent Architecture
    91→
    92→The chat system uses a thin orchestrator pattern where a single top-level chat agent dispatches to specialized subagents. The knowledge graph is the communication bus — agents read from and write to the graph independently, never passing data directly between each other.
    93→
    94→```
    95→User Message
    96→  │
    97→  ├─→ Extraction Pipeline (always runs, Haiku)
    98→  │     └─→ entities/relationships → SurrealDB graph
    99→  │
   100→  └─→ Chat Agent (Sonnet, thin orchestrator)
   101→        ├─→ Direct tools (search, entity detail, decisions, observations)
   102→        └─→ Subagent dispatch
   103→              └─→ PM Agent (Haiku) → suggestions, observations → graph
   104→```
   105→
   106→**Two paths to the graph:**
   107→| Source | Path | Why |
   108→|--------|------|-----|
   109→| User messages | Extraction pipeline (Haiku infers entities from unstructured text) | User input is unstructured |
   110→| Agent output | Direct graph write (agents already have structured form) | Nothing to extract |
   111→
   112→**Key files:**
   113→- `chat/handler.ts` — `runChatAgent()`: streams chat agent responses with tool use
   114→- `chat/context.ts` — `buildChatContext()` / `buildSystemPrompt()`: loads graph context, builds chat agent system prompt
   115→- `chat/tools/index.ts` — `createChatAgentTools()`: registers all chat agent tools
   116→- `chat/tools/types.ts` — `ChatToolExecutionContext`: actor-typed context (`chat_agent | mcp | pm_agent`)
   117→
   118→### Chat Agent Tools
   119→
   120→| Tool | Purpose |
   121→|------|---------|
   122→| `search_entities` | Search workspace entities by text query |
   123→| `get_entity_detail` | Fetch entity with relationships and provenance |
   124→| `get_project_status` | Project task/decision/question aggregation |
   125→| `get_conversation_history` | Load recent conversation messages |
   126→| `create_provisional_decision` | Draft a decision for user review |
   127→| `confirm_decision` | Finalize a decision (requires explicit user auth) |
   128→| `resolve_decision` | Mark a decision as resolved |
   129→| `check_constraints` | Validate decision constraints |
   130→| `create_observation` | Create observation for risks/conflicts/signals |
   131→| `acknowledge_observation` | Mark observation as reviewed |
   132→| `resolve_observation` | Close a resolved observation |
   133→| `invoke_pm_agent` | Delegate to PM subagent |
   134→
   135→### Product Manager Subagent
   136→
   137→The PM agent (`agents/pm/`) is the single authority on tasks, features, and project status. It is invoked by the chat agent via `invoke_pm_agent` tool with an intent:
   138→
   139→| Intent | When to use |
   140→|--------|-------------|
   141→| `plan_work` | User discusses goals, features, or work to be done |
   142→| `check_status` | User asks about project status, progress, or blockers |
   143→| `organize` | User wants to restructure or re-prioritize |
   144→| `track_dependencies` | User asks about blocked items or dependency chains |
   145→
   146→**Key files:**
   147→- `agents/pm/agent.ts` — `runPmAgent()`: generates text with PM tools, parses strict JSON output
   148→- `agents/pm/prompt.ts` — `buildPmSystemPrompt()`: loads workspace projects and observations
   149→- `agents/pm/tools.ts` — `createPmTools()`: search_entities, get_project_status, create_observation, suggest_work_items
   150→- `agents/pm/suggest-work-items.ts` — Embedding-based semantic dedup (>0.97 exact duplicate, ≥0.8 merge, <0.8 new)
   151→
   152→**PM output schema:** `{ summary, suggestions: WorkItemSuggestion[], updated, discarded, observations_created }`
   153→
   154→The chat agent renders PM suggestions as `WorkItemSuggestionList` component blocks in the chat UI.
   155→
   156→### Observation Entity
   157→
   158→Observations (`observation/*`) are lightweight cross-cutting signals that agents write to the graph. They enable async agent-to-agent communication without forcing signals into wrong entity types.
   159→
```

It uses the AI SDK's `ToolLoopAgent` class and composes shared tools from `chat/tools/`. It is invoked by the chat agent via `invoke_pm_agent` tool with an intent:\n\n| Intent | When to use |\n|--------|-------------|\n| `plan_work` | User discusses goals, features, or work to be done |\n| `check_status` | User asks about project status, progress, or blockers |\n| `organize` | User wants to restructure or re-prioritize |\n| `track_dependencies` | User asks about blocked items or dependency chains |\n\n**Key files:**\n- `agents/pm/agent.ts` — `runPmAgent()`: creates `ToolLoopAgent` with PM tools, returns structured JSON output\n- `agents/pm/prompt.ts` — `buildPmSystemPrompt()`: loads workspace projects and observations\n- `agents/pm/tools.ts` — `createPmTools()`: composes shared tools (search_entities, get_project_status, create_observation, suggest_work_items, create_work_item)\n\n**PM output schema:** `{ summary, suggestions: WorkItemSuggestion[], updated, discarded, observations_created }`\n\nThe chat agent renders PM suggestions as `WorkItemSuggestionList` component blocks in the chat UI."
}

     1→import { ToolLoopAgent, Output, stepCountIs } from "ai";
     2→import { z } from "zod";
     3→import { ENTITY_CATEGORIES, ENTITY_PRIORITIES, type ExtractedEntity, type ExtractedRelationship } from "../../../shared/contracts";
     4→import type { ChatToolDeps, ChatToolExecutionContext } from "../../chat/tools/types";
     5→import { buildPmSystemPrompt } from "./prompt";
     6→import { createPmTools } from "./tools";
     7→
     8→const workItemSuggestionSchema = z.object({
     9→  kind: z.enum(["task", "feature", "project"]),
    10→  title: z.string().min(1),
    11→  rationale: z.string().min(1),
    12→  category: z.enum(ENTITY_CATEGORIES).optional(),
    13→  project: z.string().optional(),
    14→  priority: z.enum(ENTITY_PRIORITIES).optional(),
    15→  possible_duplicate: z
    16→    .object({
    17→      id: z.string().min(1),
    18→      name: z.string().min(1),
    19→      similarity: z.number().min(0).max(1),
    20→    })
    21→    .optional(),
    22→});
    23→
    24→const pmAgentResultSchema = z.object({
    25→  summary: z.string().min(1),
    26→  suggestions: z.array(workItemSuggestionSchema),
    27→  updated: z.array(
    28→    z.object({
    29→      existing_id: z.string().min(1),
    30→      title: z.string().min(1),
    31→      changes: z.string().min(1),
    32→    }),
    33→  ),
    34→  discarded: z.array(
    35→    z.object({
    36→      title: z.string().min(1),
    37→      reason: z.string().min(1),
    38→    }),
    39→  ),
    40→  observations_created: z.array(z.string().min(1)),
    41→});
    42→
    43→export type WorkItemSuggestion = z.infer<typeof workItemSuggestionSchema>;
    44→export type PmAgentResult = z.infer<typeof pmAgentResultSchema>;
    45→
    46→export type PmAgentOutput = PmAgentResult & {
    47→  extracted_entities: ExtractedEntity[];
    48→  extracted_relationships: ExtractedRelationship[];
    49→};
    50→
    51→export type PmAgentInput = {
    52→  deps: ChatToolDeps & { pmModel: any };
    53→  context: ChatToolExecutionContext;
    54→  intent: "plan_work" | "check_status" | "organize" | "track_dependencies";
    55→  conversationContext: string;
    56→  project?: string;
    57→};
    58→
    59→const INTENT_INSTRUCTIONS: Record<PmAgentInput["intent"], string> = {
    60→  check_status: "Primary action: call get_project_status when a project scope is available.",
    61→  plan_work: "Primary action: propose tasks/features/projects with suggest_work_items or create_work_item, deduping each item.",
    62→  track_dependencies: "Primary action: identify blockers/dependencies and create observations for high-risk paths.",
    63→  organize: "Primary action: organize work into clear, deduplicated next steps.",
    64→};
    65→
    66→export async function runPmAgent(input: PmAgentInput): Promise<PmAgentOutput> {
    67→  const system = await buildPmSystemPrompt({
    68→    surreal: input.deps.surreal,
    69→    workspaceRecord: input.context.workspaceRecord,
    70→  });
    71→
    72→  const agent = new ToolLoopAgent({
    73→    id: "pm-agent",
    74→    model: input.deps.pmModel,
    75→    instructions: system,
    76→    tools: createPmTools(input.deps),
    77→    output: Output.object({ schema: pmAgentResultSchema }),
    78→    experimental_context: input.context,
    79→    stopWhen: stepCountIs(6),
    80→  });
    81→
    82→  const result = await agent.generate({
    83→    prompt: [
    84→      "You are handling a PM request.",
    85→      `Intent: ${input.intent}`,
    86→      input.project ? `Project hint: ${input.project}` : "Project hint: not provided",
    87→      INTENT_INSTRUCTIONS[input.intent],
    88→      "Context:",
    89→      input.conversationContext,
    90→    ].join("\n"),
    91→  });
    92→
    93→  if (!result.output) {
    94→    throw new Error("pm agent did not produce structured output");
    95→  }
    96→
    97→  const extracted_entities: ExtractedEntity[] = [];
    98→  const extracted_relationships: ExtractedRelationship[] = [];
    99→
   100→  for (const step of result.steps) {
   101→    for (const toolResult of step.toolResults) {
   102→      const res = toolResult.output as Record<string, unknown> | undefined;
   103→      if (res) {
   104→        if (Array.isArray(res.extracted_entities)) {
   105→          extracted_entities.push(...(res.extracted_entities as ExtractedEntity[]));
   106→        }
   107→        if (Array.isArray(res.extracted_relationships)) {
   108→          extracted_relationships.push(...(res.extracted_relationships as ExtractedRelationship[]));
   109→        }
   110→      }
   111→    }
   112→  }
   113→
   114→  return {
   115→    ...result.output,
   116→    extracted_entities,
   117→    extracted_relationships,
   118→  };
   119→}
   120→

    53→
    54→  const surrealUrl = requireEnv("SURREAL_URL");
    55→  const surrealUsername = requireEnv("SURREAL_USERNAME");

    83→function requireEnv(name: string): string {
    84→  const value = Bun.env[name];
    85→  if (!value || value.trim().length === 0) {
    86→    throw new Error(`${name} is required`);
    87→  }
    88→
    89→  return value;
    90→}
    91→
    92→function parsePositiveInteger(value: string, envName: string): number {
    93→  const parsed = Number(value);
    94→  if (!Number.isInteger(parsed) || parsed < 1) {
    95→    throw new Error(`${envName} must be a positive integer`);
    96→  }
    97→  return parsed;
    98→}
    99→
   100→function parseUnitInterval(value: string, envName: string): number {
   101→  const parsed = Number(value);
   102→  if (!Number.isFinite(parsed) || parsed < 0 || parsed > 1) {
   103→    throw new Error(`${envName} must be a number between 0 and 1`);
   104→  }
   105→
   106→  return parsed;
   107→}
   108→
   109→function parseOpenRouterReasoning(): OpenRouterReasoningOptions | undefined {
   110→  const effortValue = Bun.env.OPENROUTER_REASONING_EFFORT;
   111→  const maxTokensValue = Bun.env.OPENROUTER_REASONING_MAX_TOKENS;
   112→
   113→  if (!effortValue && !maxTokensValue) {
   114→    return undefined;
   115→  }
   116→
   117→  const reasoning: OpenRouterReasoningOptions = {};
   118→
   119→  if (effortValue) {
   120→    const allowedEfforts: OpenRouterReasoningEffort[] = ["xhigh", "high", "medium", "low", "minimal", "none"];
   121→
   122→    if (!allowedEfforts.includes(effortValue as OpenRouterReasoningEffort)) {
   123→      throw new Error("OPENROUTER_REASONING_EFFORT must be one of xhigh, high, medium, low, minimal, none");
   124→    }
   125→    reasoning.effort = effortValue as OpenRouterReasoningEffort;
   126→  }
   127→
   128→  if (maxTokensValue) {
   129→    const parsed = Number(maxTokensValue);
   130→    if (!Number.isFinite(parsed) || parsed < 1) {
   131→      throw new Error("OPENROUTER_REASONING_MAX_TOKENS must be a positive number");
   132→    }
   133→    reasoning.max_tokens = parsed;
   134→  }
   135→
   136→  return reasoning;
   137→}
   138→

    11→export function createDeterministicIdGenerator(seed: string): () => string {
    12→  let counter = 0;
    13→  return () => {
    14→    const hash = createHash("sha256").update(`${seed}:${counter++}`).digest("hex");
    15→    return `${hash.slice(0, 8)}-${hash.slice(8, 12)}-${hash.slice(12, 16)}-${hash.slice(16, 20)}-${hash.slice(20, 32)}`;
    16→  };
    17→}
    18→

     1→import appHtml from "../../client/index.html";
     2→import { withRequestLogging } from "../http/request-logging";
     3→import { jsonResponse } from "../http/response";
     4→import { logInfo } from "../http/observability";
     5→import { createSseRegistry } from "../streaming/sse-registry";
     6→import { createRuntimeDependencies } from "./dependencies";
     7→import { loadServerConfig } from "./config";
     8→import type { ServerDependencies } from "./types";
     9→import { ensureDefaultWorkspaceProjectScope } from "../workspace/workspace-scope";
    10→import { createWorkspaceRouteHandlers } from "../workspace/workspace-routes";
    11→import { createChatIngressHandlers } from "../chat/chat-ingress";
    12→import { createEntitySearchHandler } from "../entities/entity-search-route";
    13→import { createGraphRouteHandler } from "../graph/graph-route";

    18→import { createGitHubWebhookHandler } from "../webhook/github-webhook-route";
    19→import { createFeedRouteHandler } from "../feed/feed-route";
    20→
    21→export async function startServer(): Promise<void> {
    22→  const config = loadServerConfig();
    23→  const runtime = await createRuntimeDependencies(config);
    24→  await ensureDefaultWorkspaceProjectScope(runtime.surreal);
    25→
    26→  const deps: ServerDependencies = {
    27→    config,
    28→    surreal: runtime.surreal,
    29→    assistantModel: runtime.assistantModel,
    30→    extractionModel: runtime.extractionModel,
    31→    pmModel: runtime.pmModel,
    32→    embeddingModel: runtime.embeddingModel,
    33→    sse: createSseRegistry(),
    34→  };
    35→
    36→  const workspaceHandlers = createWorkspaceRouteHandlers(deps);
    37→  const chatHandlers = createChatIngressHandlers(deps);
    38→  const entitySearchHandler = createEntitySearchHandler(deps);
    39→  const graphHandler = createGraphRouteHandler(deps);
    40→  const entityDetailHandler = createEntityDetailHandler(deps);
    41→  const entityActionsHandler = createEntityActionsHandler(deps);
    42→  const workItemAcceptHandler = createWorkItemAcceptHandler(deps);
    43→  const branchConversationHandler = createBranchConversationHandler(deps);
    44→  const githubWebhookHandler = createGitHubWebhookHandler(deps);
    45→  const feedHandler = createFeedRouteHandler(deps);
    46→
    47→  const server = Bun.serve({
    48→    port: config.port,
    49→    idleTimeout: 0,
    50→    routes: {
    51→      "/healthz": {
    52→        GET: withRequestLogging("GET /healthz", "GET", async () => jsonResponse({ status: "ok" }, 200)),
    53→      },
    54→      "/api/workspaces": {
    55→        POST: withRequestLogging("POST /api/workspaces", "POST", (request) => workspaceHandlers.handleCreateWorkspace(request)),
    56→      },
    57→      "/api/workspaces/:workspaceId/bootstrap": {
    58→        GET: withRequestLogging(
    59→          "GET /api/workspaces/:workspaceId/bootstrap",
    60→          "GET",
    61→          (request) => workspaceHandlers.handleWorkspaceBootstrap(request.params.workspaceId),
    62→        ),
    63→      },
    64→      "/api/workspaces/:workspaceId/sidebar": {
    65→        GET: withRequestLogging(
    66→          "GET /api/workspaces/:workspaceId/sidebar",
    67→          "GET",
    68→          (request) => workspaceHandlers.handleWorkspaceSidebar(request.params.workspaceId),
    69→        ),
    70→      },
    71→      "/api/workspaces/:workspaceId/conversations/:conversationId": {
    72→        GET: withRequestLogging(
    73→          "GET /api/workspaces/:workspaceId/conversations/:conversationId",
    74→          "GET",
    75→          (request) =>
    76→            workspaceHandlers.handleWorkspaceConversation(
    77→              request.params.workspaceId,
    78→              request.params.conversationId,
    79→            ),
    80→        ),
    81→      },
    82→      "/api/chat/messages": {
    83→        POST: withRequestLogging("POST /api/chat/messages", "POST", (request) => chatHandlers.handlePostChatMessage(request)),
    84→      },
    85→      "/api/chat/stream/:messageId": {
    86→        GET: withRequestLogging("GET /api/chat/stream/:messageId", "GET", (request) =>
    87→          chatHandlers.handleChatStream(request.params.messageId),
    88→        ),
    89→      },
    90→      "/api/entities/search": {
    91→        GET: withRequestLogging("GET /api/entities/search", "GET", (request) => entitySearchHandler(new URL(request.url))),
    92→      },
    93→      "/api/graph/:workspaceId": {
    94→        GET: withRequestLogging("GET /api/graph/:workspaceId", "GET", (request) =>
    95→          graphHandler(request.params.workspaceId, new URL(request.url)),
    96→        ),
    97→      },
    98→      "/api/entities/:entityId": {
    99→        GET: withRequestLogging("GET /api/entities/:entityId", "GET", (request) =>
   100→          entityDetailHandler(request.params.entityId, new URL(request.url)),
   101→        ),
   102→      },
   103→      "/api/entities/:entityId/actions": {
   104→        POST: withRequestLogging("POST /api/entities/:entityId/actions", "POST", (request) =>
   105→          entityActionsHandler(request.params.entityId, request),
   106→        ),
   107→      },
   108→      "/api/workspaces/:workspaceId/conversations/:parentId/branch": {
   109→        POST: withRequestLogging(
   110→          "POST /api/workspaces/:workspaceId/conversations/:parentId/branch",
   111→          "POST",
   112→          (request) =>
   113→            branchConversationHandler(
   114→              request.params.workspaceId,
   115→              request.params.parentId,
   116→              request,
   117→            ),
   118→        ),
   119→      },
   120→      "/api/workspaces/:workspaceId/work-items/accept": {

   133→      },
