   142→      "/*": appHtml,
   143→    },
   144→  });

   146→  logInfo("server.started", "Brain app server started", {

   150→    extractionModelId: config.extractionModelId,

     1→import { existsSync, mkdirSync, readFileSync, writeFileSync } from "node:fs";
     2→import { join } from "node:path";
     3→import { createHash, randomUUID } from "node:crypto";
     4→import { evalite } from "evalite";
     5→import { afterAll, beforeAll } from "vitest";
     6→import { suggestionGroundingScorer } from "./scorers/suggestion-grounding";
     7→import { noGenericSuggestionsScorer } from "./scorers/no-generic-suggestions";
     8→import { suggestionCountScorer } from "./scorers/suggestion-count";
     9→import type { SuggestionGoldenCase, SuggestionsEvalOutput } from "./types";
    10→import { processChatMessage } from "../app/src/server/chat/chat-processor";
    11→import type { ServerDependencies } from "../app/src/server/runtime/types";
    12→import {
    13→  type EvalRuntime,
    14→  setupEvalRuntime,
    15→  teardownEvalRuntime,
    16→  seedWorkspace,
    17→  seedUserMessage,
    18→  createEventCollector,
    19→} from "./eval-test-kit";
    20→
    21→const assistantModel = process.env.ASSISTANT_MODEL ?? "unknown";
    22→
    23→const cacheDir = process.env.EVAL_CACHE_DIR ?? "eval-results/cache";
    24→const cachePath = join(cacheDir, "suggestions-cache.json");
    25→const resultCache = loadCache(cachePath);
    26→const cases = JSON.parse(
    27→  readFileSync(join(process.cwd(), "evals", "data", "suggestion-cases.json"), "utf8"),
    28→) as SuggestionGoldenCase[];
    29→
    30→let runtime: EvalRuntime;
    31→
    32→beforeAll(async () => {
    33→  runtime = await setupEvalRuntime("suggestions");
    34→}, 120_000);
    35→
    36→afterAll(async () => {
    37→  await teardownEvalRuntime(runtime);
    38→}, 120_000);
    39→
    40→evalite<SuggestionGoldenCase, SuggestionsEvalOutput, SuggestionGoldenCase>("Onboarding Suggestions Golden Cases", {
    41→  data: cases.map((testCase) => ({ input: testCase, expected: testCase })),
    42→  task: async (input) => runCase(input),
    43→  scorers: [
    44→    suggestionGroundingScorer,
    45→    noGenericSuggestionsScorer,
    46→    suggestionCountScorer,
    47→  ],
    48→  columns: ({ input, output, scores }) => [
    49→    { label: "Model", value: assistantModel },
    50→    { label: "Case", value: input.id },
    51→    { label: "ExpectedMin", value: input.expectedMinSuggestions ?? 1 },
    52→    { label: "Returned", value: output.suggestions.length },
    53→    { label: "Grounded", value: formatScoreCell(scoreByName(scores, "suggestion-grounding")) },
    54→    { label: "NoGeneric", value: formatScoreCell(scoreByName(scores, "no-generic-suggestions")) },
    55→    { label: "Count", value: formatScoreCell(scoreByName(scores, "suggestion-count")) },
    56→  ],
    57→});
    58→
    59→function scoreByName(
    60→  scores: Array<{ name: string; score: number | null }>,
    61→  name: string,
    62→): number {
    63→  const match = scores.find((score) => score.name === name);
    64→  if (!match || match.score === null || Number.isNaN(match.score)) {
    65→    throw new Error(`Missing score for scorer: ${name}`);
    66→  }
    67→
    68→  return match.score;
    69→}
    70→
    71→function formatScoreCell(value: number): string {
    72→  return value.toFixed(2);
    73→}
    74→
    75→async function runCase(testCase: SuggestionGoldenCase): Promise<SuggestionsEvalOutput> {
    76→  const cacheKey = buildCaseCacheKey(assistantModel, testCase);
    77→  const cached = resultCache[cacheKey];
    78→  if (cached) {
    79→    return cached;
    80→  }
    81→
    82→  const { workspaceRecord, conversationRecord } = await seedWorkspace(runtime.surreal);
    83→  const userMessageRecord = await seedUserMessage(runtime.surreal, conversationRecord, testCase.input);
    84→  const messageId = randomUUID();
    85→
    86→  await runtime.surreal.query(
    87→    "UPDATE $workspace SET onboarding_turn_count += 1, updated_at = $now;",
    88→    { workspace: workspaceRecord, now: new Date() },
    89→  );
    90→
    91→  const sseStub = createEventCollector();
    92→  sseStub.registerMessage(messageId);
    93→
    94→  const deps: ServerDependencies = {
    95→    config: runtime.config,
    96→    surreal: runtime.surreal,
    97→    assistantModel: runtime.assistantModel,
    98→    extractionModel: runtime.extractionModel,
    99→    embeddingModel: runtime.embeddingModel,
   100→    sse: sseStub,
   101→  };
   102→
   103→  await processChatMessage({
   104→    deps,
   105→    conversationId: conversationRecord.id as string,
   106→    messageId,
   107→    workspaceRecord,
   108→    userMessageRecord,
   109→    userText: testCase.input,
   110→  });
   111→
   112→  const events = sseStub.getEvents(messageId);
   113→  const assistantEvent = events.find((e) => e.type === "assistant_message") as
   114→    | { type: "assistant_message"; text: string; suggestions?: string[] }
   115→    | undefined;
   116→
   117→  if (!assistantEvent) {
   118→    const errorEvent = events.find((e) => e.type === "error") as
   119→      | { type: "error"; error: string }
   120→      | undefined;
   121→    throw new Error(
   122→      `No assistant_message event for case ${testCase.id}; ` +
   123→      `events: ${events.map((e) => e.type).join(", ")}` +
   124→      (errorEvent ? `; error: ${errorEvent.error}` : ""),
   125→    );
   126→  }
   127→
   128→  const output: SuggestionsEvalOutput = {
   129→    caseId: testCase.id,
   130→    input: testCase.input,
   131→    assistantText: assistantEvent.text,
   132→    suggestions: assistantEvent.suggestions ?? [],
   133→  };
   134→
   135→  resultCache[cacheKey] = output;
   136→  saveCache(cachePath, resultCache);
   137→  return output;
   138→}
   139→
   140→function loadCache(path: string): Record<string, SuggestionsEvalOutput> {
   141→  if (!existsSync(path)) {
   142→    return {};
   143→  }
   144→
   145→  return JSON.parse(readFileSync(path, "utf8")) as Record<string, SuggestionsEvalOutput>;
   146→}
   147→
   148→function saveCache(path: string, cache: Record<string, SuggestionsEvalOutput>): void {
   149→  mkdirSync(cacheDir, { recursive: true });
   150→  writeFileSync(path, JSON.stringify(cache, null, 2));
   151→}
   152→
   153→function buildCaseCacheKey(modelId: string, testCase: SuggestionGoldenCase): string {
   154→  const cacheVersion = "suggestions-v6";
   155→  const caseHash = createHash("sha256").update(JSON.stringify(testCase)).digest("hex").slice(0, 24);
   156→  return `${cacheVersion}:${modelId}:${testCase.id}:${caseHash}`;
   157→}
   158→
```

     1→import { readFileSync } from "node:fs";
     2→import { join } from "node:path";
     3→import { randomUUID, createHash } from "node:crypto";
     4→import { createOpenRouter } from "@openrouter/ai-sdk-provider";
     5→import { RecordId, Surreal } from "surrealdb";
     6→import type { StreamEvent } from "../app/src/shared/contracts";
     7→import type { SseRegistry } from "../app/src/server/streaming/sse-registry";
     8→import type { ServerConfig } from "../app/src/server/runtime/config";
     9→import type { WorkspaceSeedItem } from "./types";
    10→
    11→export function createDeterministicIdGenerator(seed: string): () => string {
    12→  let counter = 0;
    13→  return () => {
    14→    const hash = createHash("sha256").update(`${seed}:${counter++}`).digest("hex");
    15→    return `${hash.slice(0, 8)}-${hash.slice(8, 12)}-${hash.slice(12, 16)}-${hash.slice(16, 20)}-${hash.slice(20, 32)}`;
    16→  };
    17→}
    18→
    19→export type EvalRuntime = {
    20→  surreal: Surreal;
    21→  extractionModel: any;
    22→  embeddingModel: any;
    23→  assistantModel: any;
    24→  config: ServerConfig;
    25→  namespace: string;
    26→  database: string;
    27→};
    28→
    29→const surrealUrl = process.env.SURREAL_URL ?? "ws://127.0.0.1:8000/rpc";
    30→const surrealUsername = process.env.SURREAL_USERNAME ?? "root";
    31→const surrealPassword = process.env.SURREAL_PASSWORD ?? "root";
    32→const schemaPath = join(process.cwd(), "schema", "surreal-schema.surql");
    33→
    34→export async function setupEvalRuntime(suiteName: string): Promise<EvalRuntime> {
    35→  const namespace = `eval_${Date.now()}_${Math.floor(Math.random() * 100000)}`;
    36→  const database = `${suiteName}_${Math.floor(Math.random() * 100000)}`;
    37→
    38→  const surreal = new Surreal();
    39→  await surreal.connect(surrealUrl);
    40→  await surreal.signin({ username: surrealUsername, password: surrealPassword });
    41→
    42→  await surreal.query(`DEFINE NAMESPACE ${namespace};`).catch((error) => {
    43→    if (!isAlreadyExistsError(error)) throw error;
    44→  });
    45→  await surreal.use({ namespace, database });
    46→  await surreal.query(`REMOVE DATABASE ${database};`).catch(() => undefined);
    47→  await surreal.query(`DEFINE DATABASE ${database};`).catch((error) => {
    48→    if (!isAlreadyExistsError(error)) throw error;
    49→  });
    50→  await surreal.use({ namespace, database });
    51→
    52→  const schemaSql = readFileSync(schemaPath, "utf8");
    53→  await surreal.query(schemaSql).catch((error) => {
    54→    if (!isAlreadyExistsError(error)) throw error;
    55→  });
    56→
    57→  const openRouterApiKey = requireEnv("OPENROUTER_API_KEY");
    58→  const extractionModelId = requireEnv("EXTRACTION_MODEL");
    59→  const assistantModelId = requireEnv("ASSISTANT_MODEL");
    60→  const embeddingModelId = requireEnv("OPENROUTER_EMBEDDING_MODEL");
    61→  const embeddingDimension = Number(requireEnv("EMBEDDING_DIMENSION"));
    62→  const extractionStoreThreshold = Number(process.env.EXTRACTION_STORE_THRESHOLD ?? "0.3");
    63→  const extractionDisplayThreshold = Number(process.env.EXTRACTION_DISPLAY_THRESHOLD ?? "0.5");
    64→
    65→  const openrouter = createOpenRouter({ apiKey=[REDACTED] });
    66→  const extractionModel = openrouter(extractionModelId, { plugins: [{ id: "response-healing" }] });
    67→  const assistantModel = openrouter(assistantModelId, { plugins: [{ id: "response-healing" }] });
    68→  const embeddingModel = openrouter.textEmbeddingModel(embeddingModelId);
    69→
    70→  const config: ServerConfig = {
    71→    openRouterApiKey,
    72→    assistantModelId,
    73→    extractionModelId,
    74→    embeddingModelId,
    75→    embeddingDimension,
    76→    extractionStoreThreshold,
    77→    extractionDisplayThreshold,
    78→    surrealUrl,
    79→    surrealUsername,
    80→    surrealPassword,
    81→    surrealNamespace: namespace,
    82→    surrealDatabase: database,
    83→    port: 0,
    84→  };
    85→
    86→  return {
    87→    surreal,
    88→    extractionModel,
    89→    embeddingModel,
    90→    assistantModel,
    91→    config,
    92→    namespace,
    93→    database,
    94→  };
    95→}
    96→
    97→export async function teardownEvalRuntime(runtime: EvalRuntime): Promise<void> {
    98→  await runtime.surreal.query(`REMOVE DATABASE ${runtime.database};`).catch(() => undefined);
    99→  await runtime.surreal.query(`REMOVE NAMESPACE ${runtime.namespace};`).catch(() => undefined);
   100→  await runtime.surreal.close().catch(() => undefined);
   101→}
   102→
   103→export async function seedWorkspace(surreal: Surreal, workspaceName?: string, nextId?: () => string): Promise<{
   104→  workspaceRecord: RecordId<"workspace", string>;
   105→  workspaceName: string;
   106→  projectRecord: RecordId<"project", string>;
   107→  conversationRecord: RecordId<"conversation", string>;
   108→  ownerPersonCount: number;
   109→}> {
   110→  const id = nextId ?? randomUUID;
   111→  const now = new Date();
   112→  const resolvedWorkspaceName = workspaceName ?? `Eval ${Date.now()}`;
   113→  const workspaceRecord = new RecordId("workspace", id());
   114→  const projectRecord = new RecordId("project", id());
   115→  const conversationRecord = new RecordId("conversation", id());
   116→  const ownerRecord = new RecordId("person", id());
   117→
   118→  await surreal.create(workspaceRecord).content({
   119→    name: resolvedWorkspaceName,
   120→    status: "active",
   121→    onboarding_complete: false,
   122→    onboarding_turn_count: 0,
   123→    onboarding_summary_pending: false,
   124→    onboarding_started_at: now,
   125→    created_at: now,
   126→    updated_at: now,
   127→  });
   128→
   129→  await surreal.create(projectRecord).content({
   130→    name: "Eval Project",
   131→    status: "active",
   132→    created_at: now,
   133→    updated_at: now,
   134→  });
   135→
   136→  // NOTE: Do NOT link default eval project to workspace via has_project.
   137→  // Only workspace_seed projects should appear in workspace scope so the
   138→  // extraction LLM sees the correct project list for project-vs-feature classification.
   139→
   140→  await surreal.create(ownerRecord).content({
   141→    name: "Marcus",
   142→    created_at: now,
   143→    updated_at: now,
   144→  });
   145→
   146→  await surreal.relate(ownerRecord, new RecordId("member_of", id()), workspaceRecord, {
   147→    role: "owner",
   148→    added_at: now,
   149→  }).output("after");
   150→
   151→  await surreal.create(conversationRecord).content({
   152→    createdAt: now,
   153→    updatedAt: now,
   154→    workspace: workspaceRecord,
   155→    source: "onboarding",
   156→  });
   157→
   158→  return { workspaceRecord, workspaceName: resolvedWorkspaceName, projectRecord, conversationRecord, ownerPersonCount: 1 };
   159→}
   160→
   161→export async function seedConversationContext(
   162→  surreal: Surreal,
   163→  conversationRecord: RecordId<"conversation", string>,
   164→  context: Array<{ role: "user" | "assistant"; text: string }>,
   165→  nextId?: () => string,
   166→): Promise<string[]> {
   167→  const id = nextId ?? randomUUID;
   168→  const now = Date.now();
   169→  const messageIds: string[] = [];
   170→  for (const [index, message] of context.entries()) {
   171→    const seededMessageRecord = new RecordId("message", id());
   172→    await surreal.create(seededMessageRecord).content({
   173→      conversation: conversationRecord,
   174→      role: message.role,
   175→      text: message.text,
   176→      createdAt: new Date(now + index),
   177→    });
   178→    messageIds.push(seededMessageRecord.id as string);
   179→  }
   180→  return messageIds;
   181→}
   182→
   183→export async function seedUserMessage(
   184→  surreal: Surreal,
   185→  conversationRecord: RecordId<"conversation", string>,
   186→  text: string,
   187→  nextId?: () => string,
   188→): Promise<RecordId<"message", string>> {
   189→  const messageRecord = new RecordId("message", (nextId ?? randomUUID)());
   190→  await surreal.create(messageRecord).content({
   191→    conversation: conversationRecord,
   192→    role: "user",
   193→    text,
   194→    createdAt: new Date(),
   195→  });
   196→  return messageRecord;
   197→}
   198→
   199→export async function loadWorkspacePeopleCount(
   200→  surreal: Surreal,
   201→  workspace: RecordId<"workspace", string>,
   202→): Promise<number> {
   203→  const [people] = await surreal
   204→    .query<[Array<{ id: RecordId<"person", string> }>]>(
   205→      "SELECT id FROM person WHERE id IN (SELECT VALUE `in` FROM member_of WHERE out = $workspace);",
   206→      { workspace },
   207→    )
   208→    .collect<[Array<{ id: RecordId<"person", string> }>]>();
   209→  return people.length;
   210→}
   211→
   212→
   213→export async function seedGraphEntities(
   214→  surreal: Surreal,
   215→  workspaceRecord: RecordId<"workspace", string>,
   216→  projectRecord: RecordId<"project", string>,
   217→  conversationRecord: RecordId<"conversation", string>,
   218→  seeds: WorkspaceSeedItem[],
   219→  nextId?: () => string,
   220→): Promise<void> {
   221→  const id = nextId ?? randomUUID;
   222→  const now = new Date();
   223→  const seedMessageRecord = new RecordId("message", id());
   224→  await surreal.create(seedMessageRecord).content({
   225→    conversation: conversationRecord,
   226→    role: "assistant",
   227→    text: "Workspace seed context.",
   228→    createdAt: new Date(now.getTime() - 60_000),
   229→  });
   230→
   231→  for (const seed of seeds) {
   232→    const entityRecord = new RecordId(seed.kind, id());
   233→    await surreal.create(entityRecord).content(buildSeedEntityContent(seed.kind, seed.text, now));
   234→
   235→    await surreal.relate(seedMessageRecord, new RecordId("extraction_relation", id()), entityRecord, {
   236→      confidence: 0.95,
   237→      extracted_at: now,
   238→      created_at: now,
   239→      model: "seed",
   240→      from_text: seed.text,
   241→      evidence: seed.text,
   242→      evidence_source: seedMessageRecord,
   243→    }).output("after");
   244→
   245→    if (seed.kind === "feature") {
   246→      await surreal.relate(projectRecord, new RecordId("has_feature", id()), entityRecord as RecordId<"feature", string>, {
   247→        added_at: now,
   248→      }).output("after");
   249→    }
   250→
   251→    if (seed.kind === "task" || seed.kind === "decision" || seed.kind === "question") {
   252→      await surreal.relate(entityRecord, new RecordId("belongs_to", id()), projectRecord, {
   253→        added_at: now,
   254→      }).output("after");
   255→    }
   256→
   257→    if (seed.kind === "project") {
   258→      await surreal.relate(workspaceRecord, new RecordId("has_project", id()), entityRecord as RecordId<"project", string>, {
   259→        added_at: now,
   260→      }).output("after");
   261→    }
   262→  }
   263→}
   264→
   265→function buildSeedEntityContent(kind: string, text: string, now: Date): Record<string, unknown> {
   266→  const base = { created_at: now, updated_at: now };
   267→  if (kind === "project") return { ...base, name: text, status: "active" };
   268→  if (kind === "feature") return { ...base, name: text, status: "active" };
   269→  if (kind === "task") return { ...base, title: text, status: "open" };
   270→  if (kind === "decision") return { ...base, summary: text, status: "extracted" };
   271→  return { ...base, text, status: "open" };
   272→}
   273→
   274→export function createEventCollector(): SseRegistry & { getEvents(messageId: string): StreamEvent[] } {
   275→  const events = new Map<string, StreamEvent[]>();
   276→  return {
   277→    registerMessage(messageId: string) {
   278→      events.set(messageId, []);
   279→    },
   280→    handleStreamRequest(_messageId: string): Response {
   281→      throw new Error("handleStreamRequest not available in eval mode");
   282→    },
   283→    emitEvent(messageId: string, event: StreamEvent) {
   284→      events.get(messageId)?.push(event);
   285→    },
   286→    getEvents(messageId: string): StreamEvent[] {
   287→      return events.get(messageId) ?? [];
   288→    },
   289→  };
   290→}
   291→
   292→function isAlreadyExistsError(error: unknown): boolean {
   293→  return error instanceof Error && error.message.includes("already exists");
   294→}
   295→
   296→function requireEnv(name: string): string {
   297→  const value = process.env[name];
   298→  if (!value || value.trim().length === 0) {
   299→    throw new Error(`Missing required environment variable: ${name}`);
   300→  }
   301→  return value;
   302→}
   303→
```
